paṇṭait tamiḻaka varalāṟu

patippurai
19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyum, 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkap pakutiyum tamiḻukkum, tamiḻiṉattiṟkum pukaḻpūtta poṟ kālamākum.  tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ mīṭṭuruvākkattiṟkum, tamiḻiṉa mīṭcikkum vittūṉṟiya kālam.  tamiḻmaṟumalarcci varalāṟṟil ōr ellaik kal.
ikkālac cūḻaliltāṉ tamiḻaiyum, tamiḻiṉattaiyum uyirāka vum mūccākavum koṇṭa aruntamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷum, talaivarkaḷum tōṉṟi moḻikkum, iṉattiṟkum perum paṅkāṟṟiṉar. ip poṟkālattiltāṉ aṟiñar mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ 6.12.1900il tōṉṟi 8.5.1980il maṟaintār.  vāḻnta kālam 80 āṇṭukaḷ.  tirumaṇam ceyyāmal tuṟavu vāḻkkai mēṟkoṇṭu tamiḻ muṉivarāka vāḻntu arai nūṟṟāṇṭukku mēl ariya tamiḻp paṇi ceytu maṟaintavar.  tamiḻkūṟum nallulakam vaṇaṅkattakkavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittum tamiḻiṉam taṉṉai uṇarvataṟkum, talainimirvataṟkum, āyvāḷarkaḷ āyvup paṇiyil mēlāyvai mēṟkoḷvataṟkum vaḻikāṭṭuvaṉavākum.  āyvunōkkil virinta pārvaiyuṭaṉ tamiḻukku aḻiyāta aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai vaippāka vaittuc ceṉṟavar.  tamiḻ - tamiḻariṉ aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai mīṭṭeṭuttuttanta toltamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ varicaiyil kuṟippiṭattakkavar.  tamiḻmaṇṇil 1937-1938il naṭanta mutal inti etirppup pōrai muṉṉeṭuttucceṉṟa talaivarkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ varicaiyil ivarum oruvar. varalāṟu, ilakkiyam, kalai, camayam toṭarpāṉa āyvu nūlkaḷaiyum, potunalaṉ toṭarpāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum, paṉmukap pārvaiyuṭaṉ eḻutiyavar.  
pērāciriyar muṉaivar vī. aracu avarkaḷ eḻutiya cākittiya akātemi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa ‘intiya ilakkiyac ciṟpikaḷ’  nūlvaricaiyil mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi paṟṟiya varalāṟṟu nūlil āvaṇappaṇi, varalāṟu eḻutu paṇi, kalai varalāṟu, karuttu nilai ākiya poruḷkaḷil ivartam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipula aṟiviṉai mika āḻamākap pativuceytuḷḷār.
‘muṟaiyāṉa tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟai iṉi eḻutuvataṟku etirkāla āyvāḷarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭic ceṉṟavar’ - eṉpār kā. cuppiramaṇiyapiḷḷai avarkaḷ. ‘mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi āṇṭil iḷaiyavarāka iruntālum, ārāyccit tuṟaiyil mutiyavar, nalloḻukkam vāyntavar. nallōr kūṭṭuṟavaip poṉṉē pōl pōṟṟiyavar.’ eṉṟu cuvāmi vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār avarkaḷum, “eṇpatāṇṭu vāḻntu, taṉip perum tuṟavupūṇṭu, piṟar pukāta āyvuccūḻalil pukuntu tamiḻ vaḷartta, ulakac camayaṅkaḷaiyum, kalveṭṭu kāṭṭum varalāṟukaḷaiyum, ciṟpam uṇarttum kalaikaḷaiyum tōyntu āyntu tōlā nūlkaḷ eḻutiya ārāycci aṟiñar mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaṭkut tamiḻp pēravaic cemmal eṉṟa paṭṭattiṉai vaḻaṅkiyum, tamiḻc cemmalkaḷ pēravaiyiṉ ōr uṟuppiṉarāka ēṟṟukkoṇṭum, maturaik kāmarācar palkalaikkaḻakam pārāṭṭic ciṟappikkiṟatu” eṉṟu  ip peruntamiḻ aṟiñarai appalkalaik kaḻakam pōṟṟiyuḷḷatai maṉattil koṇṭu ivariṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟōm.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka tamiḻ ilakkiyattuṟait talaivar
vī. aracu avarkaḷ - pōṟṟutalukkum, pukaḻukkum uriya ivvārāyccip pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum poruḷvaḻip pirittu,  eṅkaḷukkuk koṭuttu utaviyatuṭaṉ, patippāciriyarāka iruntum, vaḻikāṭṭiyum, ivvārāyccit tokutikaḷai āyvāḷarkaḷum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷum ciṟanta payaṉpeṟum nōkkil veḷiyiṭuvataṟku pallāṟṟāṉum utaviṉār.   avarukku em naṉṟi.  ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷai aṉaivarum vāṅkip payaṉaṭaiya vēṇṭukiṟōm.
ivvārāycci nūlkaḷ ellā vakaiyilum ciṟappōṭu veḷi varuvataṟku utaviya aṉaivarkkum naṉṟi.
- patippāḷar


tamiḻ ilakkiyavaralāṟu
 kiṟittavamum tamiḻum
1936 ām āṇṭil mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ eḻutiya mutal nūl kiṟittavamum tamiḻum eṉum innūlākum. ‘kiṟittavarāl tamiḻmoḻikku uṇṭāṉa naṉmaikaḷaik kūṟum nūl’ eṉpatu innūlukku ivar koṭuttuḷḷa tuṇait talaippu ākum. airōppiyarkaḷ varukaiyōṭu kiṟittuvam tamiḻnāṭṭil evvakaiyāṉa  paṇpāṭṭut tākkaṅkaḷai uruvākkiyatu eṉpatē innūliṉ pāṭuporuḷāka amaikiṟatu. tamiḻccūḻalil ceyyuḷ marapē perum vaḻakkāka iruntapōtu ammarapiṟku iṇaiyāṉa urainaṭai vaṭivattai kālaṉiya celvākkē namakku uruvākkiyatu. itil kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷiṉ paṅkaḷippu mutaṉmaiyāṉatu. ittaṉmai kuṟittu virivāṉa pativai innūlil mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. ceyyuḷ naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷ orucilarāl maṭṭumē vācikkappaṭum cūḻal iruntatu. āṉāl urai naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷaip paravalāka eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟavarkaḷ aṉaivarum vācikkamuṭintatu. ittaṉmai kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷāl evvakaiyil paravalākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatai mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷār.
ōlaiccuvaṭiyil irunta namatu nūlkaḷ accuvaṭivam peṟṟataṉ varalāṟṟaiyum ataṉmūlam ēṟpaṭṭa tākkaṅkaḷaiyum innūlil pativu ceytuḷḷār. rāparṭ ṭi nopili, vīramāmuṉivar. cīkaṉpālku, ellīs, irēṉiyas, pōp, kālṭuvel, percival, ṭeylar, kiḷārk, irāṭlar, viṉculō, turu ākiya airōppiyarkaḷ tamiḻukkuc ceyta paṇi kuṟitta vivaraṅkaḷai innūlil pativuceytuḷḷār.
kālaṉiyattiṉ mūlam uruvāṉa putiya taṉmaikaḷ namatu paṇpāṭṭil palvēṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuvantaṉa. atil mutaṉmaiyāṉatu accup paṇpāṭē eṉṟu collalām. ivvakaiyāṉa accup paṇpāṭṭai uruvākkiyavarkaḷ kiṟittuva pātiriyārkaḷ eṉṟāl mikaiyillai. ivvaralāṟṟai innūl ciṟappākavē pativu ceytuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
ittokuppukaḷ uruvākkattil toṭakka kālattil utaviya āyvāḷarkaḷ mā. apirāmi, pa. caravaṇaṉ ākiyōrukkum ittokutikaḷ accākum pōtu piḻaittiruttam ceytu utaviya āyvāḷarkaḷ vi. tēvēntiraṉ, nā. kaṇṇatācaṉ ākiyōrukkum naṉṟi.
ceṉṉai - 96
ēpral 2010			taṅkaḷ
vī. aracu
tamiḻp pērāciriyar
tamiḻ ilakkiyattuṟai
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakam



aṟiñar mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi
“aintaṭikku uṭpaṭṭa kuṟaḷ vaṭivam; paḷapaḷakkum vaḻukkait talai; veṇmai paṭarnta puruvaṅkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭum akaṉṟa neṟṟi; kaṉavu kāṇum kaṇṇimaikaḷaik koṇṭa vaṭṭa mukam; eṭuppāṉa mūkku; paṭapaṭaveṉap pēcat tuṭikkum mellutaṭukaḷ; kaṇukkāl teriyak kaṭṭiyirukkum nāṉku muḻa veḷḷai vēṭṭi; kālar illāta muḻukkaic caṭṭai; caṭṭaip paiyil mūkkuk kaṇṇāṭi; pavuṇṭaṉ pēṉā; kaḻuttaic cuṟṟi mārpiṉ irupuṟamum toṅkum mēl uttarīyam; iṭatu karattil toṅkik koṇṭirukkum puttakap pai. ippaṭiyāṉa tōṟṟattuṭaṉ ceṉṉai miyūciyattai aṭutta kaṉṉimārā laipreriyai viṭṭu vēkamāka naṭantu veḷiyē varukiṟārē! avartāṉ mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ.”
eḻuttāḷar nāraṇa. turaikkaṇṇaṉ avarkaḷiṉ mēṟkaṇṭa vivarippu, aṟiñar mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷaik kaṇmuṉ kāṇum kāṭci aṉupavattait tarukiṟatu.  tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷāmal, illaṟat tuṟaviyāka vāḻntavar.  eṇpatāṇṭu vāḻkkaik kālattil, aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷ muḻumaiyākat tamiḻiyal āyvup paṇikku otukkiyavar.  
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil pala putiya taṉmaikaḷ naṭaimuṟaikku vantaṉa. accu entirattaip paravalākap payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppu uruvāṉatu. cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu accukkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷ māṟṟap paṭṭaṉa. itaṉmūlam puttaka uruvākkam, itaḻiyal uruvākkam, nūl patippu ākiya pala tuṟaikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. ik kālaṅkaḷiltāṉ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ paravalāka aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.  ilakkiya, ilakkaṇap piratikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭataippōl, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tolpaḻaṅkālam kuṟittum pala putiya kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ veḷivantaṉa. pirittāṉiyar kaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai pala putiya varalāṟṟut taravukaḷai veḷikkoṇṭu vantatu. pārampariyac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. tolleḻuttukkaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.  palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷ paṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu vācikkappaṭṭaṉa.  tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ eḻuttumuṟai, ilakkiya, ilakkaṇa uruvākkamuṟai ākiyavai kuṟittu, intak kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ mūlam putitāka aṟiyappaṭṭatu.  akaḻvāyvukaḷ vaḻipeṟappaṭṭa kācukaḷ putiya ceytikaḷai aṟiya  aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintaṉa. vaṭakku, teṟku eṉa intiyāviṉ paṇpāṭṭup purital cintucamaveḷi akaḻvāyvu mūlam putiya vivātaṅkaḷukku vaḻikaṇṭatu.
tamiḻakac cūḻalil, tolporuḷ āyvukaḷ vaḻi pala putiya kūṟukaḷ uṟutippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.  āticcanallūr, arikkamēṭu akaḻ vāyvukaḷ; tamiḻakattiṉ palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟukku muntaiya kālap poruṭkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ ākiyavai tamiḻaka varalāṟṟaip putiya talaimuṟaiyil eḻutuvataṟku aṭikōliṉa. mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ mēlē vivarikkappaṭṭa cūḻaliltāṉ, tamatu āyvup paṇiyait toṭaṅkiṉār.
vēṅkaṭacāmi cuyamariyātai iyakkac cārpāḷarāka vāḻvait toṭaṅkiṉār.  piṉṉar pauttam, camaṇam ākiya camayaṅkaḷ kuṟitta akkaṟai uṭaiyavarāka iruntār. ivvakai maṉanilaiyōṭu, tamiḻc cūḻalil uruvāṉa putiya nikaḻvukaḷaik kuṟittu āyvuceyyat toṭaṅkiṉār.  kiṟittavam, pauttam, camaṇam ākiya camayaṅkaḷ, tamiḻiyalukkuc ceyta paṇikaḷaip pativu ceytār. ivvakaip pativukaḷ tamiḻil putiya tuṟaikaḷai aṟimukappaṭuttiṉa.  putiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ mūlam, tamiḻc camūkap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷai eḻutiṉār.  caṅka ilakkiyap piratikaḷ, pirāmi kalveṭṭukaḷ, piṟa kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai varalāṟu eḻutuvataṟkut taravukaḷākak koṇṭār. kalaikaḷiṉmītu īṭupāṭu uṭaiya maṉa nilaiyiṉarākavē vēṅkaṭacāmi iḷamai mutal iruntār. tamiḻk kalai varalāṟṟai eḻutum paṇiyilum tammai īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭār. kaṭṭaṭam, ciṟpam, ōviyam toṭarpāṉa ivaratu āyvukaḷ, tamiḻc camūka varalāṟṟukkup putiya varavāka amaintaṉa.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālaṅkaḷil intiyaviyal eṉṟa vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟum pēcappaṭṭatu.  intiya viyalait tirāviṭa iyalākap paṭippaṭiyāka aṭaiyāḷap paṭuttum ceyal uruppeṟṟatu.  ip paṇiyil tammai muḻumaiyāka īṭupaṭutti yavar vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ.  iṉṟu, tirāviṭa iyal tamiḻiyalāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu.  iv vaḷarccikku vittiṭṭa pala aṟiñarkaḷuḷ vēṅkaṭacāmi mutaṉmaiyāṉa paṅkaḷippāḷar āvār. 
mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya - intiya ilakkiyac ciṟpikaḷ mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi eṉṟa nūlai cākittiya akātemikkāka eḻutumpōtu ittokuti kaḷai uruvākkiṉēṉ. appōtu avaṟṟai veḷiyiṭa naṇparkaḷ vē. iḷaṅkō, ār. irājārāmaṉ ākiyōr tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar. āṉāl atu naṭaipeṟavillai. attokutikaḷ ippōtu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷaṅkaṇi patippakam pāvalar pāvēntar pāratitācaṉiṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷaiyum orē vīccil ‘pāvēntam’ eṉum talaippil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatai tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.  anta varicaiyil mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷiṉ uḻaippāl viḷainta aṟivut tēṭalkaḷai orē vīccil poruḷvaḻip pirittu muḻumaimikka paṭaippukaḷāka 1998il  uruvākkiṉēṉ. ataṉai veḷiyiṭa iḷaṅkaṇip patippakam ippōtu muṉvantuḷḷatu.  itaṉaip pārāṭṭi makiḻkiṟēṉ.  tamiḻarkaḷ ittokutikaḷai vāṅkip payaṉpeṟuvar eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
- vī. aracu


mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi āyvukaḷ
-	cuyamariyātai iyakka itaḻkaḷil ceytik kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutuvatait tamatu toṭakka eḻuttup payiṟciyāka ivar koṇṭiruntār. atu ivaruṭaiya kaṇṇōṭṭa vaḷarcciyil kuṟippiṭattakka celvākkaic celuttiyuḷḷatu.
-	kiṟittava capaikaḷiṉ varukaiyāl tamiḻil uruvāṉa navīṉa vaḷarccikaḷaip pativu ceyyum vakaiyil tamatu mutal nūlai ivar uruvākkiṉār.  tamiḻ urainaṭai, tamiḻ accu nūl pōṉṟa tuṟaikaḷ toṭarpāṉa āvaṇam atuvākum.
-	pauttam tamiḻukkuc ceyta paṅkaḷippai matippīṭu ceyyum nilaiyil ivaratu aṭuttak kaṭṭa āyvu vaḷarntatu.  pauttak kataikaḷ moḻiyākkam maṟṟum tokuppu, putta jātakak katait tokuppu, kautama puttar vāḻkkai varalāṟu eṉṟa pala nilaikaḷil pauttam toṭarpāṉa āyvup paṅkaḷippai vēṅkaṭacāmi ceytuḷḷār.
-	camaṇa camayam mītu īṭupāṭu uṭaiyavarāka vēṅkaṭacāmi iruntār. maṇimēkalai, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, ākiyavaṟṟai āyvatiṉ mūlam tamiḻc cūḻalil camaṇa varalāṟṟai āyvu ceytuḷḷār.  camaṇa camaya aṭippaṭaikaḷai virivākap pativu ceytuḷḷār.  camaṇac ciṟpaṅkaḷ, kuṟitta ivaratu āyvu taṉit taṉmaiyāṉatu.
-	palvēṟu cācaṉaṅkaḷ putitākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.  ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷiliruntu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ accu vākaṉam ēṟiṉa. intap piṉpulattil ki.mu. 5 mutal ki.mu. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu muṭiya uḷḷa tamiḻc camūkattiṉ āṭci varalāṟṟai ivar āyvu ceytār.  pallava maṉṉarkaḷ mūvar kuṟitta taṉittaṉi nūlkaḷaip paṭaittār.  itil tamiḻakac ciṟpam maṟṟum kōyil kaṭṭaṭakkalai varalāṟṟaiyum āyvu ceytār.
-	aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷaic coṟpoḻivai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu,  tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ kāla āyvilum ivar akkaṟai celuttiṉār.  tolkāppiyam, cilappatikāram kuṟitta kāla āyvil ca. vaiyāpurippiḷḷai pōṉṟōr karuttai maṟuttu āyvu nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.  ic coṟpoḻiviṉ iṉṉoru pakutiyāka caṅkak kālac camūkam toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷilum kavaṉam celuttiṉār.
-	ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷaic coṟpoḻivil cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai āyvup poruḷākkiṉār. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka ki.pi. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku  muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟaip pala nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar, pala kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟitta virivāṉa āyvai vēṅkaṭacāmi nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.  itaṉ toṭarcciyākak kaḷappirar toṭarpāṉa āyvaiyum ceytuḷḷār.  iv vāyppukaḷiṉ oru pakutiyāka aṉṟaiya tolleḻuttukkaḷ kuṟitta kaḷa āyvu cārntu, āyvukaḷai mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār.
-	ovvoru tēciya iṉattiṟkum ataṉ pārampariyac ceḻumai kuṟitta aṟiyum taravukaḷ tēvaippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟai āvaṇap paṭuttuvatu mikavum avaciyamākum. maṟaintu pōṉavaṟṟait tēṭum muyaṟci atil mukkiyamāṉatākum.  ip paṇiyaiyum vēṅkaṭacāmi mēṟkoṇṭirun tār.  ariya taravukaḷai ivar namakku āvaṇappaṭuttit tantuḷḷār.
-	tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kalai varalāṟṟai eḻutuvatil vēṅkaṭacāmi akkaṟai celuttiṉār.  pala ariya takavalkaḷai ilakkiyam maṟṟum cācaṉaṅkaḷ vaḻi tokuttuḷḷār.  avaṟṟaik kuṟittu cārpu nilaiyil niṉṟu āyvu ceytuḷḷār.  āyvāḷarukkuriya nērmai, vivēkam, kōpam ākiyavaṟṟai ivvāyvukaḷil kāṇalām.
-	patippu, moḻipeyarppu ākiya paṇikaḷilum vēṅkaṭacāmi īṭupaṭṭatai aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
-	ivaratu āyvup pātaiyiṉ cuvaṭukaḷaik kāṇumpōtu, tamiḻiyal toṭarpāṉa āvaṇappaṭuttam, tamiḻarukkāṉa varalāṟṟu varaivu, tamiḻt tēciya iṉattiṉ kalai varalāṟu maṟṟum avaikaḷ kuṟitta ivaratu karuttu nilai ākiya ceyalpāṭukaḷai nām kāṇalām.

tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
kuṟippu: mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi avarkaḷ kiṟittavamum tamiḻum (1936) eṉṉum talaippil eḻutiya nūl.

niṉaivu

eṉakkuk kalviyaṟivaip pukaṭṭit tamiḻccuvai ūṭṭiyaruḷiya eṉ arumait tamaiyaṉār mayilai cīṉi. kōvintarācaṉār avarkaḷiṉ niṉaivu malarāka innūl ilaṅkuka.
						     - nūlāciriyar.



muṉṉurai
(mutaṟpatippu) 
tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccipeṟṟa muḻu varalāṟṟaiyum aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṉum ārvam eṉakku neṭunāḷāka uṇṭu. ākaiyāl, camayam vāyttapōtellām attuṟaiyil ārāycci ceytuvantēṉ. ivvāṟirukkuṅkāl, 1934 -ām āṇṭiṉ kaṭaiciyil, vittuvāṉ tiru. te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār avarkaḷ, M.A., B.L., ceṉṉaiyiṟ kūṭṭiya tamiḻ mānāṭṭil, ceṉṉaik kiṟittavak kallūrit tamiḻppērāciriyarākiya tiru. ca.ta. caṟkuṇar avarkaḷ, B.A., “kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum” eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru coṟpoḻivu nikaḻttiṉārkaḷ. ataṉaik kēṭṭapōtu, tamiḻmoḻic carittira ārāycciyil karuttaic celuttiniṉṟa eṉakkup putiyatōr ūkkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kiṟittuva camayattavarāl tamiḻmoḻi aṭainta naṉmaikaḷaik kūṟum tamiḻnūl itukāṟum illāmaiyāl, mutalil itaṉai eḻutuvatu tāṉ nalam eṉakkaruti, ittuṟaiyil eṉ karuttaic celutti ārāycciceyyat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. itaṉiṭaiyil 1935 -ām āṇṭiṉ kaṭaiciyil, ilaṅkai tirumiku. vipulānanta cuvāmikaḷ, B.Sc., (London), ceṉṉaikku vantiruntapoḻutu, avarkaḷiṭam eṉatu ārāycciyait terivittēṉ. cuvāmikaḷ, itu avaciyam eḻutavēṇṭiya pakutitāṉ eṉṟu terivittu, ivvārāycci campantamākat taṅkaḷ karuttukkaḷaiyum terivittu eṉṉai ūkkappaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. ituvē ipputtakam eḻutappaṭṭataṉ varalāṟu. innūliluḷḷa kuṟṟaṅkaḷai nīkkik kuṇaṅkaḷaiyē koḷḷumpaṭi periyōrai vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
innūlukku mukavurai eḻutiyaruḷumāṟu ilaṅkai tirumiku. vipulānanta cuvāmikaḷaik kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. cuvāmikaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ eḻutiyaruḷiṉārkaḷ.  itaṟkiṭaiyil putitoru yōcaṉai tōṉṟiyatu. tamiḻ ilaṅkaikkum tamiḻ intiyāvukkum tamiḻt talaimai pūṇṭu viḷaṅkum tirumiku. cuvāmikaḷ aruḷiya mukavurai yoṉṟē cālum eṉṟālum, innūl intu ulakattukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, kiṟittuva ulakattukkum uriyatākaliṉ, kiṟittuvat tamiḻaṟiñariṉ mukavuraiyum innūlukku avaciyam eṉat tōṉṟiyatu. kiṟittuva matattil kattōlik, puroṭṭasṭeṇṭu eṉṉu irupirivukaḷ iruppatāl, avviru pirivukaḷiṉ cārpāka iraṇṭu tamiḻp periyōrkaḷai mukavurai eḻutiyutavumāṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷuk kiṇaṅki, mēl nāṭṭu moḻikaḷ kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟil pulamai vāyntu, tamiḻc coṟpiṟappārāycci ceytuvanta periyār, yāḻppāṇattu nallūr uyartiru. cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar avarkaḷ, O.M.I., oru nūṉmukam eḻuti aruḷiṉārkaḷ. avvāṟē, eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅki, taṅkaḷ uttiyōka kālam pōka mikutiyuḷḷa kālamellām, taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil, yātoru ūtiyamum karutāmal māṇavarkaḷukkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip pāṭañcolli, avarkaḷaic ciṟanta tamiḻppulavarāka viḷaṅkac ceytuvarukiṟa naṟkuṇap periyār, ceṉṉaik kiṟittuvak kallūrit tamiḻp pērāciriyar tiru. ca.ta. caṟkuṇar avarkaḷ, B.A., āṅkila mukavurai eḻutiyaruḷiṉārkaḷ. ākavē, innūl mūṉṟu mukavuraikaḷuṭaṉ vecaḷivarukiṟatu. inta mukavuraikaḷai eḻutiyaruḷiya mūṉṟu periyārkaḷukkum eṉatu maṉamārnta vantaṉam uriyatākum.
innūliṟ cērppataṟkāka, tiru. va. cuppaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ, ṭākṭar ji.yū. pōp aiyar, ṭākṭar kālṭvel aiyar, paṭaṅkaḷaik koṭuttutaviyataṟkāka avarkaḷukkum eṉatu vantaṉam uriyatākum.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉai, 24-11-36.	mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉurai
(iraṇṭām patippu) 
ic ciṟu nūlaip peritum matittu, nallurai eḻutiya periyār palarkkum, naṉkoṭai utaviya ceṉṉaip pāṭacālaip puttaka ilakkiya caṅkattārkkum, 1940 ām āṇṭiṉ iṇṭermīṭiyeṭ parīṭcaikkup pāṭamāka vitittaruḷiya palkalaikkaḻakap periyōrkkum peritum vantaṉam kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉai, 10-7-38.	mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉurai
(mūṉṟām patippu) 
iraṇṭām patippu veḷivantu pattu āṇṭukaḷāy viṭṭaṉa. ippōtu innūl mūṉṟām patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. muṟpatippu kaḷil, āṅkāṅku irunta cila coṟkaḷai nīkki avaṟṟiṟkup patilākat tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷaic cērntiruppatum, iraṇṭoru iṭaṅkaḷil putitākac cila ceytikaḷaic cērttiruppatum, tavira, atikamāka vēṟu karuttukkaḷai itil cērkkavillai. tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār, ippatippaic cemmaipeṟa nalla muṟaiyil acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyataṟkāka avarkaḷukku eṉatu vantaṉam uriyatākum.
mayilāppūr,
ceṉṉai, navampar, 48.	mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi

muṉṉurai
(nāṉkāmpatippu) 
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum eṉṉum innūl ippōtu nāṉkām patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. ippatippil persival aiyar, teylar aiyar, pavurī caiyar, kiḷārk aiyar, rāṭlar aiyar, uviṉslō aiyar, turu aiyar, pavar aiyar eṉpavarkaḷaip paṟṟiya ciṟukuṟippum, ekara, okara eḻuttukkaḷaip paṟṟiya piṉṉiṇaippum putitākac cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattār, innāṉkām patippai nalla muṟaiyil acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyataṟkāka avarkaḷukku eṉatu vantaṉattait terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
mayilāppūr,			
ceṉṉai-4,		      mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmi
20-5-55.

mukavurai I
innūlāciriyar āṇṭil iḷaiyarāyiṉum ārāyccit tuṟaiyil mutirntavar. teṉṉintiyāvilum īḻa nāṭṭilum aṇmaiyil vāḻnta tamiḻaṟiñar palaruṭaiya vāḻkkai varalāṟu, aṉṉā riyaṟṟiya nūl varalāṟu eṉṉum ivaitammait teḷivupeṟa ārāyntaṟiyavēṇṭumeṉṉum peruviruppa muṭaiyavar. pala āṇṭukaḷāka nāṉ ivarai naṉkaṟivēṉ; nalloḻukkam vāyntavar; nallōruṭaiya kūṭṭuṟavaip poṉṉēpōṟ pōṟṟupavar.
innūliṉakattē ivar tokuttuvaittirukkiṉṟa muṭipukaḷ ivarāṟ pala āṇṭukaḷāka ārāyntu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. palappala āṅkila nūlkaḷilum, tamiḻnūlkaḷilum, parantukiṭanta uṇmaikaḷai oruṅku tiraṭṭic cevvi peṟa vakuttu, nūluruvamākki veḷiyiṭ ṭirukkiṉṟār. innūl kallūrimāṇavarkkum, tamiḻaṟiñarkkum, teṉṉintiya carittira ārāycciyāḷarkkum iṉṟiyamaiyāta toṉṟākum. ātaliṉ, itaṉait tamiḻulakam uvantēṟṟu ātarikku meṉpataṟ kaiyamillai. in nūlāciriyar iṉṉum pala ārāyccikaḷ ceytu, tamiḻakattiṟkup payaṉpaṭa vāḻumāṟu ellām valla iṟaivaṉ iṉṉaruḷ purivāṉāka.
civāṉanta vittiyālayam,
kallaṭiyuppōṭai,			cuvāmi vipulāṉantar.
kāttāṉkuṭi, cilōṉ.		 		19-11-36.

mukavurai II
orukālam mikac cīrtiruttam aṭaintu muḻuulakattiṉ cīrtiruttattukkum mēlvaric caṭṭamāyt tikaḻntirunta intiyāviṉ makārākiya nām, iṭaikkālattil ac cīrtiruttattai orupuṭai iḻantupōyiṉōm. emakku vekukālattiṉ piṉṉar nākarikam peṟṟōrākiya airōppiyarē, nām pala tuṟaikaḷiṉ maḻuṅka viṭṭirunta nākarikattai mīṇṭum aṭaiya, emakkuk kaṭavuḷ kaṭāṭcattāl tuṇaiceyvōrāṉārkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻ vaḷarccikku mikka upakāramāṉa acciyantira upāyattai em nāṭṭil varuvittatu aṉṉōr ceyta naṉṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟām. tamiḻil taṉi vāṉa nūlkaḷai ākkum iyakkattait toṭakkiviṭṭavarum, neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyāyc coṟkaḷiṉ arttattai viḷakkum vaṉappu vāynta tamiḻ akarātiyaic ceytu tantavarum airōppiya kurumārē. viññāṉattuṟaikaḷiṟ cila nūlkaḷai mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil iyaṟṟiṉōrum, tamiḻariṉ kalviyai viruttiyākkum poruṭṭu āṅkila pāṭacālaikaḷait tāpittōrum, avaṟṟiṉ mūlamāyp pala kalaikaḷukku uriya nūlkaḷait tamiḻil iyaṟṟiyum iyaṟṟuvittum tamiḻ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup pērutavi ceytōrum airōppiyak kiṟittuva camaya kuravarē. muṉṉāḷil camaṇap periyōr tamiḻiṟkut tirukkuṟaḷ, cīvakacintāmaṇi ākiya centamiḻ nūlkaḷai utaviyatupōla, piṉṉāḷil tēmpāvaṇi ākiya pattiya nūlkaḷai utaviyavarum ōr airōppiya kuravarē. it tamiḻvallunarkaḷaip piṉ toṭarntu nam nāṭṭuk kiṟittuvarkaḷum, avvak kālattē tamiḻiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukku uriya pala toṇṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar.
ivvāṟu, airōppiyarum tamiḻnāṭṭavarumāṉa kiṟittavar kaḷāl tamiḻum tamiḻulakum peṟṟukkoṇṭa pērupakāraṅkaḷaic curukkamāyk kūṟuvatāy, iḷaiñarum kirakittukkoḷḷattakka peṟṟiyāṉa tamiḻnaṭai poruntiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟa “kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum” eṉap peyar cūṭiya nūlaip pārttu āṉanta vacattarāṉōm. “tiṉaittuṇai naṉṟi ceyiṉum paṉait tuṇaiyāk koḷvar payaṉ terivār” eṉṟapaṭi, nammaṉōrellām kiṟittavar tamiḻukkuc ceyta naṉṟiyai aṟintu pārāṭṭumpaṭiyāka icciṟiya nūlaic ciṟappuṟa eḻutittanta srī mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭacāmikkum, aḻakuṟap patippitta srī. kā. ē. vaḷḷi nātaṉukkum tamiḻulakam mikak kaṭappāṭu uṭaiyatākiṟatu.
nallūr,
yāḻppāṇam.		        		  cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar.

Foreword III
The most brilliant age in Tamil literature, the so-called Sangam Age, came to a close at the downfall of the three Tamil Dynasties, and was succeeded by dark ages which continued all through the long period of anarchy and misrule in Southern India. It was after the advent of Europeans in general, and European Christian Missionaries in particular, that the revival of Tamil letters began.
When Europeans realised that the best way of knowing and influencing the Tamil people, among whom they came to live and work, was through the knowledge of their language, they applied themselves closely to the study of Tamil. They found it was a noble language with a noble literature, but was generally neglected by its people, mainly by reason of its literary dialect being almost entirely poetical, and so different from the spoken dialect, that one, who could read and write the language as spoken, could not even divide a line of poetry into its component words, unless one had made a special study of it for years.
So they seem to have thought it their first duty to try to open the eyes of the Tamils to the greatness of their language. With this object in view, many of them have expressed their opinion of Tamil, its literature, and grammar. Their opinions are very useful and interesting, but we have space here only for a few of them.
First, as regards the language : Dr. Winslow writes, “It is not perhaps extravagant to say that in its poetic from the Tamil is more polished and exact than the Greek, and, in both dialects, with its borrowed treasures, more copious than the Latin. In its fulness and power, it more resembles English and German than any other living language.” Dr. Schimid writes of Tamil that “the mode of collocating its words follows the logical or intellectual order more so than even the Latin or Greek.” The Rev. P. Percival observes, “Perhaps no language combines greater force with equal brevity; and it may be asserted that no human speech is more close and philisophic in its expression as an exponent of the mind. The sequence of things - of thought, action, and its results - is always maintained inviolate. “Professor Whitney writes that he “was informed by an American who was born in South India and grew up to speak its language vernacularly along with his English, a man of high education and unusual gifts as a preacher and writer, that he esteemed the Tamil a finer language to think and speak in than any European tongue known to him.”
Dr. G. Slater, late professor of Indian Economics in the 
University of Madras, writes that “the Tamil language is extraordinary in its subtlety and sense of logic.” and that “the perfcection with which it has been developed into an organ for precise and subtle thought, combined with the fact that it represents a much earlier stage in the evolution of infexional language than any Indo germanic tongue, suggests................the priority of the Dravidians in attaining settled order and regular Government,” and that “as it is known to us it the product of a very long period of a somewhat elaborate civilisation.”
Secondly, as regards its literature: That “unique genius,” Father Beschi, writes that “the Tamil poets use the genuine language of poetry.”and “there are excellent works in Tamil poetry on the subject of the divine attributes and the nature of virtue.” Dr. Pope, referring to the numerous ethic works in Tamil, remarks, “I have felt sometimes as if there must be a blessing in store for a people that delight so utterly in compositions thus remarkably expressive of a hunger and thirst after righteousness.” The Rev. H. Bower, a Eurasian, writes of the Tamil Poetess Auvaiyar, that “She sang like Sappho, yet not of love, but of virtue,” and of the Chintamani that “it is a moral epic of the highest merit,” and of the Sacred Kural that the “work is superior to the institutes of Manu and is worthy of the divine Plato himself.”
Thirdly, as regards its grammar: Rev. T. Brotherton wirtes that “it is generally allowed by all who are at all conversant with Tamil literature, that the Nunnool or Tamil Grammar of Pavananti, is the most philosophically and logically arranged Grammar of any 
language in the universe, and that it is a “venerable and esteemed work.”
Indian Christians have said none the less. One of them writes of the Sacred Kural and its author that “it is refreshing to think that a nation which has produced so great a man and so unique a work cannot be a hopeless, desciple race.” Another often observed that “one of the main objects of studying Tamil literature is to know and catch the genius of the ancient Tamil people” And another wrote : “Indeed, the various kinds of elliptical constructions in which Tamil greatly delights, the philosophic sequence of things that it always maintains inviolate, the logical and musical choice of terms that it often makes from a veritable host of synonyms, the implied significations that it often presents to the careful reader, the numerous and extraordinary poetic licenses that it enjoys, and, above all, the extreme elasticity by which it possesses a large amount of linguistic economy and is capable of being interpreted according to the intellectual capacity of the reader, these - these alone, which none can deny it, are likely to make any one, who is rightly and faithfully devoted to its literature, a person of no mean mental power..... There is no easier and better method of gauging the genius and mental acumen of the ancient Tamil race than to study their science of grammar which they have carried to a very high degree of prefection. Theie subtle lingusitic observation, their happy invention of the wonderful Sutra-device, their logical arrangment of the rules, and their elaborate methods of construing them cannot but be the wonder of all ages.”
After many such eulogies, European and Indian Christians have exhorted the Tamils not to neglect their language. They have also pointed out that neglect of vernacular means only national suicide. Dr. Pope wrote, “Let the Tamils cease to be ashamed of their Tamil.” Dr. Murdoch wrote to them, “Let them be assured that so long as they despise their mother tongue they have merely a thin whitewash of civilisation,” and quoted the words of Von F. Schlagel. “That acquaintance with foreign languages, whether dead or living, which is necessary for men of letters and fashion, is no longer associated with neglect of their vernacular speech, a neglact which is always sure to work its own revenge on those who practise it, and which can never be supposed to create any prejudice either in favour of their politeness or their erudition.”
Now, even if Europeans have not done anything else than trying thus to open our eyes to the excellence of our langugage, and warning us against neglecting it, we must be extremely indebted to them. But they have done more.
They simplified the Tamil script. They introduced space between the words, simple or compound, of a sentence, for it had been the native custom to write a whole sentence as if it were composed of one long word. They were the first to print Tamil tracts, books, and papers. They were the first to introduce the study of Tamil inot the civilised countries of the world. They were the first to make Tamil translations from European languages. They were the first to compile Tamil word-books, dictionaries and lexicons, which made obsolete the time-honoured custom of memorising the metrical Tamil vocabularies, before taking up literature. They were the first to write Tamil treatises on Natural Science. They were the first to urge educated Tamils to bring out expurgated editions of Tamil works, and to write interesting general literature of a wholesome character. They were the first to show us how to make a critical study of our language, literature, and grammar. And they were the first not only to teach us that “that is not good language that all understand not,” and that there can be no social, moral, religious, political, or economical progress among a people who have no homely prose literature to read, but also to write “Modern Tamil prose,” and create the reading habit among us.
The above lines contain a brief summary of a long public Tamil lecture, “Christianity and Tamil,” delivered by me about two years ago, in Madras. The author of this book, who is a broad-minded Hindu School-Master, says that the lecture roused his curiosity, and gave him the incentive to make a study of the subject himself and the book is a result of it.
The book contains a very important chapter, which is not 
generally known, in the history of Tamil Language and Literature. The short biographical sketches and notes of some of the European and native Christian Tamil Scholars, and the well-selected 
specimens of Tamil writing of some of the European scholars 
cannot fail to be quite interesting. We have here fine specimens of the flowery language of the Italian Tamil Scholar, Father De Nobilibus; of the simple, direct, and telling language of the Swiss, the Rev. C.T. E. Rhenius, on the Truthfulness of the Gospels; of the poetical and literary, and colloquial prose style of the Italian, Father C.J. Beschi, the greatest of the European Tamil Scholars; who 
expresses, in one of the passage quoted here by the author, his high appreciation of the Sacred Kural.
The book is well written too. Its language is simple, correct, natural, and free from all tricks of style.
The book is sure to make all Tamil students feel extremely proud of the literature and grammar of their language, and love and study and improve it with zeal.
The author has my great thanks, and the book, my best wishes.

Madras,		S.D. SARGUNAR
1-11-1986	Leturer in Tamil
Madras Christian College.

1. kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
1. airōppiyar varukai
kiṟittuvarākiya airōppiyariṉ toṭarpiṉāl tamiḻ moḻikku uṇṭāṉa vaḷarcciyai ārāyvatē innūliṉ nōkkam. ataṉai ārāyap pukuvataṟku muṉ airōppiyariṉ toṭarpu eppaṭi ēṟpaṭṭateṉpatait terivippatu muṟaiyākum. ākaiyāl, airōppiyar namatu tēcattiṟku vanta varalāṟṟaic curukkamāka eḻutuvōm.
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē, mēlnāṭṭār namatu intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ, ciṟappākat teṉ intiyāvuṭaṉ, vaṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat terikiṟatu. yavaṉar1 eṉṉum airōppiyak kulattār cēra nāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟkaraip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṟ paṇṭakacālaikaḷ amaittu, avaṟṟil namatu nāṭṭuc carakkukaḷaic cēmittuvaittuk kappalkaḷ mūlamāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ. maturai, pukār2 mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷilum yavaṉar vacittu vantatākat tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉāl aṟikiṟōm. ki.mu. 22-il, maturaiyil aracāṇṭirunta pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ, akastascīcar3 eṉṉum yavaṉa aracaṉiṭam tūtuvarkaḷai aṉuppiṉāṉ eṉṟu strēpo4 eṉṉum mēl nāṭṭāciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṟār. tamiḻarukkum yavaṉarukkum irunta iv vaṇikat toṭarpu ki.pi. 47 mutal mēṉmēlum atikappaṭṭatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, anta āṇṭil hippalas5 eṉpavar, intu mākaṭalil vīcukiṟa vaṭakiḻakku, teṉmēṟkup paruvakkāṟṟaik kaṇṭupiṭittār. ip paruvakkāṟṟu vīcukiṟa kālaṅkaḷiṟ pirayāṇam ceyvatāl, mālumikaḷ viraivākak kappalkaḷaic celuttik kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟakkuṟaiyak kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟ cellakkūṭiyatāyiruntatu. ākavē, yavaṉa vaṇikarkaḷ atikamākat tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varat talaippaṭṭārkaḷ. iv vaṇikap perukkattiṉāl namatu tēcattārukku ērāḷamāṉa varuvāy kiṭaittatu. “intiyā tēcam ērāḷamāṉa celvattai yavaṉa tēcattiliruntu āṇṭutōṟum kavarntu koḷkiṟatu.” eṉṟu piḷiṉi eṉṉum āciriyar eḻutiyirukkiṟār. intat tamiḻar - yavaṉar vaṇikat toṭarpaippaṟṟip puṟanāṉūṟu, maṇimēkalai, cilappatikāram mutaliya caṅkanūlkaḷilum kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻarukkum yavaṉarukkum irunta intat toṭarpu vāṇikat tōṭumaṭṭum niṉṟiruntatē tavira camayam, kalai, moḻi mutaliyavaikaḷiṟ ciṟitum iṭampeṟavillai. yavaṉar nammuṭaṉ nērmukamākak koṇṭirunta inta vaṇikattoṭarpu ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvaraiyil nilaipeṟṟiruntatu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, arapi tēcattu mukamatiyarkaḷ ekiptu, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷai veṉṟu kaippaṟṟiya piṟaku, yavaṉar namatu tēcattuṭaṉ vaittirunta nērmukamāṉa vaṇikattoṭarpu taṭaippaṭṭu viṭṭatu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku mukamatiyar kaṭalvaḻiyaiyum taraivaḻiyaiyum kaippaṟṟi ātikkam peṟṟatōṭu, intiya vāṇikattaiyum kaippaṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. intiyap poruḷkaḷai vāṅkik koṇṭupōy airōppiya tēcaṅkaḷil viṟpaṉai ceyvatāṟ perumporuḷ ūtiyaṅ kiṭaippataik kaṇṭu, mukamatiyar airōppiyarai intiyāvuṭaṉ nērmukamāka vāṇikam ceyya voṭṭātapaṭi taṭuttuviṭṭārkaḷ. ivvitamākap paṇṭaikkālattil airōppiyar namatu tēcattuṭaṉ koṇṭirunta vaṇikat toṭarpu aṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. mukammatiyar malaiyāḷak karaiyiṟ paṇṭakacālaikaḷai amaittu, miḷaku mutaliya poruḷkaḷaic coṟpa vilaikku ērāḷamāy vāṅkic cēmittuvaittu, avaṟṟaik kappalkaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu arapikkaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṅkaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu, aṅkiruntu tarai vaḻiyāka cūyas, keyrō, alaksāntiriyā mutalāṉa nakaraṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭupōy, oṉṟukkup paṉmaṭaṅku atikamāka vilaivaittuc carakkukaḷai viṟṟu, perumporuḷ tiraṭṭiṉārkaḷ. vaṇikattoḻil ceyyum kulattār eppoḻutum celvantarā yiruppatu ellōrum aṟinta uṇmai. itaṉpaṭi, viyāpārat toḻilil īṭupaṭṭa mukammatiyar mēṉmēlum celvam peṟṟuc ciṟappaṭaintu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. airōppiyar ippōtu kaittoḻil, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟil ciṟappaṭaintiruppatu pōla akkālattiṟ ciṟappup peṟāmal tāḻnta nilaiyil iruntārkaḷ. ākaiyāl, āṭai, mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷ intiyā mutaliya kīḻait tēcaṅkaḷiliruntē airōppāvukku ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭuvantaṉa. itaṉāl inta vāṇikattait taṅkaḷ kaiyil vaittirunta mukammatiyar peruñ celvam campātittatil viyappoṉṟumillai. ivvāṟu cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa.
intiyac carakkukaḷai airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṟ koṇṭu pōy viṟpataṉāl mukammatiyar celvam peṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻvataik kaṇṭa airōppiya tēcattār, tāṅkaḷum nērmukamāka intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikañ ceytu poruḷ īṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu āval koṇṭārkaḷ. āṉāl, intiyāvukkuc cellumvaḻi mukammatiyar ātikkattiliruntapaṭiyālum, vēṟu kulattār ivvaṇikattaik kaikkoḷḷātapaṭi avarkaḷ (mukammatiyar) veku karuttākak kāval ceytiruntapaṭiyālum, airōppiyariṉ eṇṇam kaikūṭavillai. akkālattil pūkōḷa nūl teriyāta paṭiyāl vēṟu vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyaiyum airōppiyar aṟintirukka villai. intiyā airōppāvukkuk kiḻakkup pakkattil irukkiṉṟa teṉpatumaṭṭum avarkaḷukkut teriyum. āṉāl, aṅkuc celvataṟku vēṟu vaḻi teriyāmal tikaittaṉar. akkālattil, airōppāk kaṇṭattil intiyāvaippaṟṟip pala kataikaḷ vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. intiyā tēcam pūlōka cuvarkkam eṉṟum, paṇam kāykkum maraṅkaḷ aṅkē ērāḷamāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tēṉ āṟum, pāl āṟum karai puraṇṭōṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum palavitamāṉa ceytikaḷ ulavivantaṉa. ivvitac ceytikaḷaik kēṭṭapōtu airōppiyarukku nāvil nīr curantatu. tēṉāṟum pālāṟum karai puraṇṭōṭum antap pūlōka cuvarkkattai, paṇam kāykkum maraṅkaḷ vaḷarukiṟa viyappāṉa intiyā tēcattaik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟum, attēcattuṭaṉ nērmukamāka vaṇikat toṭarpu peṟṟup poruḷ campātikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum airōppiyar āvalōṭu virumpiṉārkaḷ. akkālattil airōppā muḻutum intiyāviṉ pēccākavē iruntatu. āṉāl, intiyāvukku eppaṭip pōvatu? intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyai mukammatiyar kaippaṟṟit taṅkaḷ ātikkattil vaittukkoṇṭu, vēṟu kulattār avvaḻiyākac cellātapaṭi, kaṇṇai imai kāppatupōl kāttu varukaiyil, airōppiyar intu tēcam cella niṉaittatu, muṭavaṉ tēṉukku ācaippaṭṭatupōl iruntatu.
ivvāṟiruntu varukaiyil, pūkōḷa nūlaippaṟṟi ārāyntu vanta aṟivāḷikaḷ pūmi appaḷampōl taṭṭaiyāka irukka villai eṉṟum, pantupōl uruṇṭai vaṭivāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum aṟintārkaḷ. āṉāl, ippūkōḷanūl uṇmaiyai makkaḷ oppukkoḷvataṟku neṭunāḷ ceṉṟatu. kaṭaiciyāka, pōrccukal tēcattavarākiya kolampas eṉpavar, ip pūkōḷa nūl uṇmaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, intiyā tēcattiṟkuc cella vēṟoru vaḻiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikkat tuṇintār. eppaṭi eṉṟāl, pūmi uruṇṭaiyāyiruppatāl, oruvaṉ ōriṭattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēṟku mukamākap pirayāṇam ceytukoṇṭē pōṉāl, kaṭaiciyil avaṉ kiḻakkup pakkamākat tāṉ puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku vantu cērvāṉ eṉṉum pūkōḷa nūl uṇmaiyai maṉattiṟkoṇṭu, intiyā tēcam airōppāvukkuk kiḻakkil irukkiṟapaṭiyāl, airōppā viliruntu mēṟkup pakkamāyp pirayāṇam ceytu koṇṭu pōṉāl, intiyā pōyc cēralām eṉṟu kolampas eṉpavar nampiṉār. āṉāl, avar colliya karuttai oruvarum ātarikkavillai. kaṭaiciyāka, mikunta cipāriciṉ mēl, speyṉ tēcattu aracaṉ kolampasukku utavi ceyya icaintu, cila kappalkaḷaiyum, mālumikaḷaiyum pirayāṇattukku vēṇṭiya cāmāṉkaḷaiyum koṭuttutaviṉāṉ. kolampas speyṉ tēcattaiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu, mēṟkup pakkamāyk kaṭaliṟ pirayāṇam ceytu koṇṭu pōṉār. cila nāṭkaḷ pirayāṇam ceyta piṟaku, kolampas mēṟkintiya tīvukaḷaiyum, putiya ulakam eṉṉum amerikkāk kaṇṭattaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittār. āṉāl, kolampas at tīvukaḷ intiyā tēcattaic cērntavai eṉṟum, ak kaṇṭam intiyā tēcam eṉṟum tavaṟāka niṉaittār. avar tām kaṇṭupiṭitta pūpākam intiyā tēcamtāṉ eṉkiṟa nampikkaiyuṭaṉ iṟantār. āṉāl, uṇmaiyil atu intiyā tēcam aṉṟu. “oṉṟai niṉaikkiṉ atu oḻintiṭṭoṉṟākum” eṉṟapaṭi, kolampas intiyā tēcattaik kaṇṭupiṭikkum nōkkattōṭu cella, taṟceyalākap putiya pūpākaṅkaḷaik kāṇap peṟṟār. kolampas putiya ulakattaik kaṇṭu piṭitta ceyti airōppiyarkaḷukkup putiya uṇarcciyai aḷittatu. ēṉaiya airōppiya cātiyārum intiyā tēcattukkup putiya vaḻiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikka ūkkaṅkoṇṭu muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ūkkaṅkoṇṭavarkaḷil pōrccukal tēcattaracaṉum oruvaṉ. emāṉuyeci6 eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa anta aracaṉ āppirikkā kaṇṭattaic cuṟṟik koṇṭu kiḻakku mukamākap pirayāṇam ceytāl, intiyā tēcam pōyc cēralām eṉṟu nampikkai koṇṭavaṉ. ākaiyāl, avvaḻiyākac ceṉṟu intiyā tēcattaik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpaṭi vāskō-ṭā-kāmā eṉpavarai aṉuppiṉāṉ. vāskō-ṭā-kāmā pōrccukkal tēcattut tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya lispaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattai viṭṭu 1497 ām varucam cūlai mātam 9ām nāḷ puṟappaṭṭuk kaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṉṟapōtu, etirkāṟṟil akappaṭṭu aṉēka iṭar iḻippukaḷuk kuṭpaṭṭār. āṉālum, maṉan taḷarāmal mēṉmēlum kappalai yōṭṭik koṇṭu, āppirikkāk kaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyākiya naṉṉampikkai muṉaiyaic cuṟṟik kaṭantu, ak kaṇṭattiṉ kiḻakkuk karaiyōramākap pirayāṇam ceytu, kaṭaiciyil milāṇṭā7 eṉṉum kaṭaṟkaraip paṭṭiṉattai aṭaintār. atu ak kālattil pēr peṟṟa tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉam. ākaiyāl, intiyāviliruntu pala kappalkaḷ aṅku vantiruntaṉa. vāskō-ṭā-kāmā aṅkirunta oru mukammatiya mālumiyai intiyā tēcattukku vaḻi kāṭṭumpaṭi tammuṭaṉ aḻaittuk koṇṭu aṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭu, anta mukammatiya mālumiyiṉ utaviyāl, ki.pi. 1498-ām varucam mē mātam 22ām nāḷ malaiyāḷakkaraiyil uḷḷa kaḷḷik kōṭṭai8 eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattai aṭaintār. yavaṉarukkup piṟaku vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu intiyāvukku vanta kiṟittava matattaic cērnta airōppiyar ivar tām. piṟaku, vāskō-ṭā-kāmā kaḷḷikkōṭṭaiyai aracāṇṭirunta cāmuttiri aracaṉaik9 kaṇṭu pēci, pōrccukal tēcattār kaḷḷikkōṭṭaiyil varttakam ceyya avvaracaṉiṭam uttaravu peṟṟuk koṇṭu, mīṇṭum tam ūrukkuc ceṉṟu, tām intiyāvaik kaṇṭupiṭitta makiḻvuc ceytiyait tammuṭaiya aracaṉukkut terivittār. pōrccukal tēcattaracaṉ mikavum makiḻcci aṭaintu, intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ varttakam ceyvataṟkākavum, kūṭumāṉāl, at tēcattai taṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭuttuvataṟkākavum cila kappalkaḷai āyattam ceytu, pīṭrō ālvāres kāprāl10 eṉpavar talaimaiyiṟ pala pōrccukīciyarai aṉuppiṉāṉ. intak kāprāl eṉṉum talaivar kappalaic celuttikkoṇṭu pōkiṟapōtu. āppirikkāk kaṇṭattiṉ karaiyōramākac ceṉṟāl, etirkāṟṟil akappaṭṭu varuntavēṇṭiyirukkum eṉṟu niṉaittu, akkaṇṭat tukku mēṟkē atika tūramāyk kaṭaliṟ pōykkoṇṭirukkaiyil, teṉ amerikkāvil uḷḷa, ippōtu pirēcil11 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟa ceḻippāṉa tēcattil taṟceyalākap pōyc cērntār. tām pōkak karuti vanta intiyā tēcam cellāmal taṟceyalāy vēṟu oru putiya tēcattiṟ ceṉṟatai aṟintu viyappuṟṟu, avar mīṇṭum aṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭu intiyā tēcam vantu cērntār. pirēcil tēcattiliruntu intiyāvukkup puṟappaṭumpōtu aṅkiruntu cila paḻac ceṭikaḷaik koṇṭu vantu intiyāviṟ paravac ceytār. avar koṇṭu vantavai miḷakāy, muntirikkoṭṭai, koyyāppaḻam, aṉācip paḻam, cīttāppaḻam, pappāḷippaḻam mutaliyavai. ippaḻacceṭikaḷ ivar koṇṭu varuvataṟku muṉpu namatu nāṭṭilillai.
patiṉaintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kaṭaiciyil namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta paraṅkiyar (pōrccukīciyarukkup “paṟaṅkiyar” eṉṟu peyar) kocci, kaḷḷikkōṭṭai, kōvā mutaliya kaṭaṟkaraip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil amarntu vāṇikam ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku muṉ taṉi urimaiyāy vāṇikam ceytirunta mukammatiyarukkup paṟaṅkiyar vantu vāṇikam ceyvatu piṭikka villai. itaṉāl paṟaṅkiyarukkum mukammatiyarukkum cila caṇṭaikaḷ naṭantaṉa. accaṇṭaikaḷiṟ paṟaṅkiyar veṟṟi peṟṟu, mukammatiyarai aṭakki, arapikkaṭalait taṅkaḷ āṭciyiṟ koṇṭuvantārkaḷ. atumutal cumār oru nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil paṟaṅkiyarkaḷ intiyā, ilaṅkai mutaliya kīḻait tēcaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ vāṇikam ceyvatōṭu maṭṭum nillāmal, kiṟittuva matattaip paravac ceyyavum pāṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimārkaḷaik koṇṭu vantu, peruntokaiyāṉa intukkaḷaik kiṟittavarākkiṉārkaḷ. cavēriyār, tattuvapōtaka cuvāmi, jāṉ-ṭi-piriṭ ṭō, vīramā muṉivar mutaliya pātirimār ivarkaḷ aḻaittuvanta ēcuviṉ capaiyaic cērnta tuṟavikaḷāvar. ituvumaṉṟi, paṟaṅkiyar taṅkaḷ vāṇipap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷaik kāvalpuriyum poruṭṭu, paṟaṅkip pōr vīrarkaḷaiyum koṇṭuvantiruntārkaḷ. ippōrvīrarkaḷ intiyap peṇkaḷai maṇañceytukoḷḷumpaṭi kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. ivvitak kalappumaṇattiṉāṟ piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷ “tuppāci” eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya putiya kulattārāṉārkaḷ. intat tuppācikaḷ paṟaṅkimoḻiyaip pēcivantaṉar.
ivvitamākap paṟaṅkiyar intiyāvukkup putu vaḻi kaṇṭu piṭittu, intiya tēcattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytu poruḷ tiraṭṭic celvantarāvataik kaṇṭu ēṉaiya airōppiya iṉattārum intiyā tēcattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyya āval koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ hālaṇṭu12 eṉṉum nāṭṭiluḷḷa ṭaccukkārarum im muyaṟciyil muṉaintu niṉṟārkaḷ. inta ṭaccukkārarukku “ollāntar” eṉpatum peyar. inta ollāntar intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakku vaḻiyoṉṟaip putitākak kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. kolampas airōppāvukku mēṟku mukamāy intiyāvukkup pōka oru vaḻiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṉṟārallavā? paṟaṅkiyar airōppāvukkut teṟku mukamāy āppirikkāvaic cuṟṟi kiḻakku mukamākac ceṉṟu, intiyāvukku oru vaḻiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷē. ataippōlavē tāṅkaḷum airōppāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pakkamāyc ceṉṟu intiyāvukkup putuvaḻi oṉṟaik kaṇṭupiṭikkalāmē eṉṟu ollāntar niṉaittārkaḷ. appaṭi niṉaittatu akkālattil pūkōḷa cāttirattai avarkaḷ carivara aṟiyavillai eṉpatait terivikkiṟatu. inta eṇṇattōṭu avarkaḷ nāṉku kappalkaḷai intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pātaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikka aṉuppi ṉārkaḷ. inta muyaṟciyil avarkaḷ tōlviyaṭaintārkaḷ. āṉāl, muṉpu aṟintirāta cila tīvukaḷaiyum, kaṭalkaḷaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. ollāntar vaṭakiḻakkup pakkamāyp putuvaḻi kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceyta atēkālattil, vēṟu nāṉku kappalkaḷaip paṟaṅkiyar kaṇṭupiṭitta vaḻiyaip pārttuvarumpaṭi aṉuppiṉārkaḷ. ikkappalkaḷ kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷil oṉṟākiya cāvā (cāvakam) tīvuvaraiyiṟceṉṟu tirumpi vantaṉa. atumutal ollāntar intiyā tēcattuṭaṉum kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷuṭaṉum vāṇipam ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ intiya viyāpārattai mutalil taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil naṭatti vantaṉar. piṉṉar 1902 ām varucam mārccu mātam ‘2’ ām nāḷ “kiḻakkintiya varttakac caṅkam” eṉṉum pērāl oru caṅkattai ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭārkaḷ. inta ollāntar patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil paṟaṅkiyarai intiyā, ilaṅkai mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷiliruntu turattiviṭṭu, avarkaḷirunta iṭaṅkaḷait tāṅkaḷ kaippaṟṟik koṇṭu vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷum mataveṟikoṇṭu, yāḻppāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil intuk kōyilkaḷai iṭittum, intukkaḷaik kaṭṭāyappaṭuttik kiṟittavarkaḷākkiyum, kiṟittavarkaḷukku maṭṭum araciyal aluvalkaḷaik koṭuttum koṭumai ceytaṉar. mutalil vāṇikarkaḷāka vanta ivarkaḷ piṟaku nāṭukaḷaip piṭittu aracāḷa muṟpaṭṭārkaḷ. āṉāl, āṅkilēyar ivarkaḷai intiyāvilum ilaṅkaiyilumiruntu turatti viṭṭārkaḷ.
intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceytu celvan tiraṭṭa virumpiya maṟṟoru tēcattār āṅkilēyarāvar. ivarkaḷum ollāntaraip pōlavē intiyāvukku vaṭakiḻakkup pātaiyoṉṟaik kaṇṭu piṭikka mutalil muyaṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ cumār 50 āṇṭukaḷāka vaṭakiḻakkup pātaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikka muyaṟci ceytum payaṉaṭaiya villai. piṟaku  1578-ām āṇṭil, car pirāṉcis ṭirēk13 eṉṉum āṅkilēyar, intiyāviliruntu pōrccukal tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟukoṇṭirunta oru paṟaṅkik kappalaip piṭittuk koḷḷai yaṭittār. koḷḷaiyiṭukaiyil, āppirikkāviṉ naṉṉampikkai muṉaiyaic cuṟṟik koṇṭu intiyāvukkuc cellum vaḻiyaik kuṟippiṭukiṟa paṭam oṉṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttār. ataṉpiṟaku 1594- l car jāṉ laṅkāsṭ14 eṉṉum āṅkilēyar naṉṉampikkaimuṉai vaḻiyāka cāvā varaiyil yāttirai ceytu tirumpivantār. piṉṉar ki.pi.1600-ām āṇṭu ṭicampar mātam  “āṅkilēyak kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi” eṉṉum varttaka caṅkam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. atumutal āṅkilēyar intiyāvukku vantu vāṇikam ceyyat talaippaṭṭaṉar.
namatu tēcattil vāṇikam ceyya vanta iṉṉoru airōppiya iṉattār ṭēṉs15 eṉṉum ṭēṉiṣkārar. ivarkaḷ ṭeṉmārkku tēcattaic cērntavar 1620-l kiḻakkintiya varttaka caṅkattai ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, namatu tēcattil uḷḷa taraṅkampāṭi, paḻavēṟkāṭu, cirāmpūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantaṉar. āṉāl, ivarkaḷiṉ vāṇikam palappaṭavillai. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil, taraṅkampāṭi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷai ivarkaḷ āṅkilē yarukku viṟṟuviṭṭup pōyviṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷum kiṟittava matattaip paravacceyya muyaṉṟu vantaṉar.
intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceyyak kaṭaiciyāka muyaṟci ceytavarkaḷ pireñcukkārar. “pireñcuk kiḻakkintiya varttakac caṅkam” 1664-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. pireñcukkārar putuccēri mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceytuvantaṉar. ṭiyupḷē eṉpavar putuccērikku aluvalāḷarāy vantapōtu intiyāvil pireñcu aracāṭciyai nilaināṭṭa muyaṟciceytār. avaruṭaiya muyaṟci veṟṟiyākavē iruntatu. āṉāl, pireñcukkārariṉ veṟṟiyaiyum celvākkaiyum taṭaippaṭutti, avarkaḷai intiyāviliruntu turattiviṭṭu, āṅkila aracāṭciyai nilaināṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇattuṭaṉ āṅkilēyak kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyār muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. piṟaku iru tiṟattārukkum pala pōrkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. kaṭaiciyāka, āṅkilēyar veṟṟi peṟṟu āṅkila aracāṭciyai nilai niṟuviṉar. itaṟku akkālattil intiyāviṉ nilaimai iṭaṅ koṭuttatu. piṟaku, ilaṅkai mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷaiyum piṭittu aracāḷat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivaṟṟai yellām virivāka eḻutuvatu yām collap pukunta karuttukkup puṟampāṉatāku mātalāl, mikac curukkamākak kūṟappaṭṭatu.
vāṇikattukkāka vanta airōppiyarkaḷ intiyaraik kiṟittava matattiṟ cērkkavum muyaṟci ceytu vantārkaḷ. intuk kaḷaik kiṟittavarākkum poruṭṭu aṉēka pātirimārkaḷai airōppiya tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu aḻaittu vantārkaḷ. namatu tēcattukku vanta pātirimārkaḷ kiṟittava matattaip pōtikka muṟpaṭṭapōtu, avarkaḷukku innāṭṭumoḻikaḷ taṭaiyāka niṉṟaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ mutalil intiya moḻikaḷaik kaṟkavēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam ēṟpaṭṭatu. tāṅkaḷ enta nāṭṭārukku matapōtaṉai ceyyak karutiṉārkaḷō anta nāṭṭu moḻiyaik kaṟṟut tērntu, atiṟ pēcavum coṟpoḻivu ceyyavum muyaṉṟārkaḷ. anta muṟaiyil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vanta pātirimārkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyaik kaṟṟut tērntu tamiḻiṟ pēcavum, coṟpoḻivu nikaḻttavum, nūl iyaṟṟavum toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu tamiḻmoḻikkum airōppiyap pātiri mārukkum ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpiṉāl, tamiḻiṟ cila māṟutalkaḷ allatu muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟai ārāyvatu tāṉ ipputtakattiṉ nōkkamākum.
tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutal allatu vaḷarcci aṉaittum pātirimārkaḷāl uṇṭāṉavaiyē. illaṟattārākiya airōppiya uttiyōkattarkaḷāl tamiḻukku yātoṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭatākat teriyavillai. namatu nāṭṭil 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta āṅkilēya aluvalāḷar taṅkaḷ aluval muṟaikku utaviyāka iruppataṟkākavum, namatu nāṭṭuk kalaiyiṉ pōkkai aṟivataṟkākavum, “ceṉṉaik kalvic caṅkam”16 eṉṉum oru caṅkam ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭu, ataṉ mūlamākat tamiḻaik kaṟṟu vantārkaḷ. ic caṅkattiṉāl tamiḻmoḻikkuc ciṟappāka yātoru vaḷarcciyum uṇṭāṉatākac collamuṭiyātu. kiṟittavarkaḷāl tamiḻukku ēṟpaṭṭa apivirutti ellām pātirimāraic cērntatē. pātirimārum tamiḻukkākac ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttuṭaṉ ceyyavillai. taṅkaḷ matattaip parappuvataṟkākac ceyta muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉē tamiḻiṟkuc cila naṉmaikaḷai aḷittatu. evvāṟāyiṉum, anta naṉmaikaḷ airōppiyap pātirimārkaḷāl uṇṭāṉavaiyē. avaṟṟai iṉi ārāyvōm.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1. urōmar, kirēkkar.				
2. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam.
3. Augustus Caesar.				
4. Strabo.		
5. Hippalus.
6. Emanuel.
7. Milanda.			
8. Calicuti.	
9. Zamorian.
10. Pedro Alvarez Cabral.		
11. Brazil.
12. Holland.
13. Sir Francis Drake.			
14. Sir John Lancaster.
15. Danes.
16. The Madras College.

2. urainaṭai nūl varaṉmuṟai
airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉē tamiḻmoḻiyil taṉi urainaṭainūl iruntatillai. ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam, nikaṇṭu, cōtiṭam, maruttuvam mutaliya ellā nūlkaḷum ceyyuḷilē iyaṟṟappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ivaikaḷukku uraiyāciriyarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya uraikaḷait tavira taṉippaṭṭa urainaṭainūl  oṉṟēṉum iruntatillai. āṉāl, paṇṭaik kālattil urainaṭaiyum ceyyuḷumākak kalantu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai,
	“toṉmai tāṉē,
uraiyoṭu puṇarnta paḻaimai mēṟṟē”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷil  238-vatu cūttirattāl aṟiyalām. avaṟṟai “uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ” eṉpar. peruntēvaṉār pāratamum, takaṭūryāttiraiyum uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷukku utāraṇaṅkaḷākum. āṉāl, avviru nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ippōtu, urainaṭaiyum ceyyuḷumākak kalantu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭuvatu cilappatikāram oṉṟē. cilappatikāram uraiyiṭaiyiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ eṉpatai,
	“uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ
uraikā laṭikaḷ aruḷa maturaik
kūla vāṇikaṉ cāttaṉ kēṭṭaṉaṉ”
eṉṉum cilappatikārap patikac ceyyuḷaṭikaḷāl aṟiyalām. cilappatikāram uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa pōtilum, annūlil mikac ciṟu pakuti maṭṭun tāṉ (uraipeṟu kaṭṭuraiyum vāḻttuk kātaiyiṉ uraippāṭṭu maṭaiyum) urainaṭai; maṟṟap perum pakutikaḷ pāṭṭē. eṉavē, uraiyum pāṭṭumākak kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟa cilappatikārattil, araikkāṟ pakutikūṭa urainaṭai amainti rukkavillai. iṟantupaṭṭa peruntēvaṉār pāratamum, takaṭūr yāttiraiyum pōka, vaḻakkāṟṟil uḷḷa cilappatikāram oṉṟiṉait tavirttu, “uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ” nūl tamiḻil vēṟoṉṟum illai. eṉavē, taṉi urainaṭai nūlkaḷ paṇṭaik kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭavillai eṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malaipōl viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
tamiḻt tollāciriyarkaḷ pāṭṭaiyē peritum pōṟṟic ceyyuḷnaṭaiyilēyē ellā nūlkaḷaiyum iyaṟṟivaittaṉar eṉpatu uṇmai. itaṉāl, paṇṭai āciriyarkaḷukku urainaṭai eḻutat teriyātu eṉṟu niṉaikkakkūṭātu. avarkaḷ ciṟanta urainaṭaiyil nūl iyaṟṟum āṟṟal vāyntavar eṉpatiṟ caṟṟum aiyamillai. iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷiṉ uraippāyiram oṉṟē, avarkaḷ urainaṭai eḻutuvatil talaiciṟantavar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum. iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷiṉ uraippāyira urainaṭaiyiṉ aḻakaiyum iṉimaiyaiyum paṭittu iṉpuṟāta tamiḻar uṇṭō? attakaiya tīñcuvai coṭṭum urainaṭaiyiṉai iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya paṇṭait tamiḻar, taṉi urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟa niṉaittirup pārāṉāl, eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷai eḻutiyirukkak kūṭumaṉṟō? taṉi urainaṭai nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟuvatu akkālattil vaḻakkamillāta paṭiyāl, avarkaḷ urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟāmal, ellā nūlkaḷaiyum ceyyuḷilē ceytu vaittaṉar. (srīpurāṇam mutalāṉa, camaṇa camayattavarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷ uḷa. avai tamiḻ eṉṟum collakkūṭāmal camakkirutam eṉṟum collakkūṭāmal maṇippiravāḷa naṭaiyāka iruppatāl, avaṟṟait tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ eṉṟu yām koḷḷavillai.) iṅṅaṉam paṇṭait tamiḻar ceyyuḷ naṭaiyil maṭṭum nūl iyaṟṟi, urainaṭaiyil nūl iyaṟṟuvatai ēṉ puṟakkaṇittaṉar? urainaṭai eḻuta naṉkaṟintiruntum, paṇṭaik kālattil urainaṭai nūl eḻutum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭāta kāraṇam eṉṉa? avaṟṟai ārāyvōm.
ikkālattil varuttamiṉṟi eḻutuvataṟkēṟṟa karuvikaḷum poruḷkaḷum ērāḷamāka amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. kākitam, pēṉā, peṉcil mutaliya karuvikaḷ eḻutuvōrukku yātoru varutta miṉṟi utaviyāy niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, paṇṭaikkālattil eḻutukaruvikaḷ ivvaḷavu tuṇaiyāy amaintirukkavillai. paṉaiyēṭukaḷum, iruppeḻuttāṇiyun tavira, vēṟu eḻutu karuvikaḷ akkālattil iruntatillai. paṉaiyēṭukaḷil eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutuvatu, kākitattiṟ pēṉāviṉāl eḻutuvatu pōl eḷitāṉa kāriyamaṉṟu; mikak kaṭiṉamāṉa kāriyam. ākaiyāl, paṇṭaittamiḻar eḻuttu vēlaiyai evvaḷavu kuṟaikka vēṇṭumō avvaḷavum kuṟaikka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. ākavē, ‘curuṅkac collal’ eṉṉum muṟaiyaik kaikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. urai naṭaiyil nūl iyaṟṟuvatu eḻuttu vēlaiyaik kuṟaippatākātu; nērmāṟāka av vēlaiyai mikutip paṭuttuvatākum. ataṉāl tāṉ curukkamākac colluvataṟkēṟṟa ceyyuḷnaṭaiyai nam paṇṭaiyōr mēṟkoṇṭārkaḷ pōlum. iṉṉoru kāraṇamum uṇṭu. aḵteṉṉaveṉṟāl, akkālattil accup poṟiyum ataṉ payaṉākiya accupputtakamum illāta paṭiyāl, nūlkaḷaik kaṟkum māṇavar tām kaṟkum ellā nūlkaḷaiyum maṉappāṭañ ceyyavēṇṭi yiruntatōṭu, nūlkaḷaip pāṭañ collum āciriyarkaḷum tām pōtikkum nūlkaḷai maṉappāṭañ ceytirukka vēṇṭiya avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. maṉappāṭam paṇṇuvataṟku urainaṭai ēṟṟataṉṟu; ceyyuḷ naṭaitāṉ ciṟantatu. ikkāraṇat tiṉālum, paṇṭait tamiḻar nūl iyaṟṟuvataṟkuc ceyyuḷ naṭaiyai mēṟkoṇṭu, urai naṭaiyaik kaiviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu niṉaikka iṭamuṇṭākiṟatu.
ikkālattuḷḷatu pōla akkālattil eḷitāka eḻutuvataṟ kēṟṟa eḻutukaruvikaḷum poruḷkaḷum illāta paṭiyiṉālē, eḻuttu vēlaiyaic curukkik koḷvataṟkākap paṇṭaiyā ciriyar ceyyuḷ naṭaiyai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu coṉṉōm. ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyē uraiyāciriyar kūṭa cila iṭaṅkaḷil, “itaṉai vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka” eṉṟu eḻutip pōntaṉar eṉṟu niṉaikkiṟōm. kuṟippurai, arumpatavurai pōṉṟa curukka māṉa uraikaḷaip paṇṭaiyōr eḻutat tuṇintataṟkuk kāraṇamum eḻutukaruvi tuṇaipporuḷkaḷ illāk kuṟaiyeṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. tamiḻaṉṉaiyiṉ muṭimaṇiyāka viḷaṅkum cilappatikārattiṟku uraiyeḻutiya āciriyar aṭiyārkkunallār, mēṟpaṭi nūṟpatikattiṉ mutal iraṇṭaṭikaḷukku maṭṭum mika virivāṉa urai eḻutiya piṉṉar, “iṉip patikac ceyyuḷuḷ, mutalīraṭiyum pōla yāṇṭum viriyātatu uraiperuku meṉpataik karuti eṉak koḷka,” eṉa eḻuti, maṟṟa aṭikaḷukku uraiyaic curukkik koṇṭatum iṅku nōkkat takkatu. urai eḻutuvataṟkeṉṟē muṟpaṭṭavar, piṉ urai peruku meṉṟu karutic curukkik koṇṭatu, culapamāka eḻutuvataṟku ēṟṟa karuvikaḷ akkālattu illāmaiyā leṉka. ikkālattuḷḷatu pōṉṟa vacatiyāṉa eḻutukaruvikaḷ akkālattu iruntiruntāl ivvāṟu uraiyaic curukkik koṇṭirukka māṭṭāraṉṟō? ākavē, paṇṭaittamiḻar urainaṭai nūl iyaṟṟātatu ak kālattil vacatiyāṉa eḻutukaruvikaḷum accu yantiramum illāta kāraṇam paṟṟiyē eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
paṇṭai nāḷil tamiḻmoḻiyil urainaṭai nūlkaḷ iruntirukkumāṉāl, tamiḻnāṭu pala tuṟaikaḷilum iṉṉum muṉṉēṟṟamaṭaintirukkum, ēṉeṉṟāl, oru nāṭu muṉṉēṟṟa maṭaiya vēṇṭumāṉāl, annāṭṭil urainaṭai nūlkaḷ uṇṭāka vēṇṭum. mēl nāṭukaḷil neṭu nāṭkaḷukku muṉpē urainaṭai nūlkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntapaṭiyāl, annāṭṭu makkaḷ pala ceytikaḷaiyum eḷitil uṇarntu, viraivil muṉṉēṟṟamaṭaiya muṭintatu. ik karuttaiyē nītipatiyum ciṟanta tamiḻk kaviyumāyirunta māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ,
“vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷāl makkaḷ tirunta vēṇṭumēyallātu ceyyuṭkaḷaip paṭittut tiruntuvatu acāttiyam aṉṟō? airōppiya moḻikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāma lirukku māṉāl, antat tēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum naṟpāṅkum aṭain tirukkak kūṭumā? appaṭiyē nammuṭaiya tāymoḻiyil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkiṟa varaiyil, intattēcam cariyāṉa cīrtiruttam aṭaiyāteṉpatu niccayam.”
eṉṟu tām eḻutiya ‘piratāpa mutaliyār’ carittirattil eḻuti yirukkiṟār. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil perum pulavarāy viḷaṅkiya tiruttaṇikai vicākap perumāḷaiyar eṉṉum vīracaivar tām eḻutiya ‘pāla pōta ilakkaṇam’ eṉṉum nūliṉ pāyirattil, urainaṭai nūlkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyaip paṟṟik kīḻ varumāṟu eḻutukiṟār:
“ilakkaṇa ilakkiya kaṇita pūkōḷa kakōḷāti nūlkaḷai yellām, ikkālattu ittēcattai yāḷukiṉṟa iṅkilīṣkārar kaḷum, avarkaḷ vacikkum kaṇṭattiluḷḷa ēṉaiyōrkaḷum, ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟik kaṟpippatai viṭṭu, vacaṉaṅkaḷilē teḷivuṟa iyaṟṟic ciṟuvarkkuk kaṟpittukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl, ak kaṇṭattil vāḻum ciṟuvarkaḷ cila nāṭkaḷilē pala nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟup pala viṣayaṅkaḷaiyum uṇarntu pala toḻilkaḷaiyum iyaṟṟum tiṟamuṭaiyarākiṉṟaṉar.
“ittēcattār annūlkaḷai yellām ceyyuḷilē ceytu avaṟṟiṟkut tiricoṟkaḷāl uraiyiyaṟṟic ciṟuvarkkuk kaṟpittu varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ, nikaṇṭu mutaliya karuvi nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟalaṉṟik kaṟkappaṭāvām. ākavē, avaṟṟuḷ oru nūlaik kaṟpataṟku neṭu nāḷ  cellukiṉṟatu. cellavē, pala nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟuṇarntu ettoḻilkaḷaiyuñ ceyyattakkavarkaḷāvatu aritām.
“itaṉāl innāṭṭuc ciṟuvarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻnāṭkaḷ vīḻ nāṭ paṭāmaṟ cila nāṭkaḷil ap pala nūlkaḷaiyum kaṟṟaṟintu et toḻilkaḷaiyum ceyyattakka vallamai aṭaivataṟku, an nūlkaḷaiyellām ulakavaḻakkiyaṟ coṟkaḷāl uraivacaṉamākat terivuṟac ceytu kalviccālaikaḷilē upayōkamuṟum paṭi ceytal naṉṟu....”
mēlē kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷkaḷiṉāl, nāṭu nākarikam peṟṟu nalamaṭaivataṟku urainaṭai nūlkaḷē pērutaviyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, mēlaittēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum celvamum peṟṟut tikaḻvataṟku an nāṭukaḷil vacaṉa nūlkaḷ vaḻakkāṟṟiliruntu vantatum oru kāraṇam eṉpatum teḷivākiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, urainaṭai nūlkaḷiṉ payaṉ naṉku teḷivākiṟatu, iṉi, tamiḻ moḻiyil urainaṭai nūl uṇṭāṉa varalāṟṟiṉai ārāyvōm.
ettoḻilum paḻakkattiṉāṟ ciṟappaṭaikiṟatu. ik karuttup paṟṟiyē, “ cittiramum kaip paḻakkam centamiḻum nāppaḻakkam” eṉṉum āṉṟōr moḻi eḻuntatu. ippaḻamoḻi urai naṭaikkum poruntiyatē. pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻar urainaṭai nūl eḻutāmalē iruntu viṭṭapaṭiyāl, kālap pōkkil urainaṭai eḻutum āṟṟalai iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. tamiḻariṉ ik kuṟaipāṭṭiṉaip paṟṟi airōppiyar cilar kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. “tamiḻil urainaṭai eḻutum paḻakkam iṉṉum tuvakkanilaiyil irukkiṟatu. viraivākavum caramāriyākavum tamiḻiṟ kavipāṭakkūṭiya pulavarkaḷ urainaṭaiyiṟ cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷ eḻutat teriyāmalirukkiṉṟaṉar,” eṉṉum karuttuppaṭa uviṉslō tamiḻ-iṅkilīṣ akarātiyiṉ mukavuraiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. “maruttuvam, kaṇitam, ilakkaṇam, nikaṇṭu mutaliya ellā nūlkaḷum (avaṟṟiṉ uraikaḷait tavira) ceyyuḷilē iyaṟṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. urai naṭaiyil nūl iyaṟṟum vaḻakkam airōppiyariṉ toṭarpāl ēṟpaṭṭatākum” eṉṟu tamiḻaippaṟṟi marṭāk turai eḻutiyirukkiṟār. ivai uṇmaiyē, tamiḻil maṭṭumaṉṟu; ellā intiya moḻi kaḷilum urainaṭai vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. airōppiyariṉ toṭar puṇṭāṉa piṟakutāṉ eṉpatiṟ caṟṟēṉum aiyamillai.
tamiḻil taṉi urainaṭai nūl airōppiyarāl mutal mutal uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmai. eppōtu yārāl, eṉṉa urainaṭai nūl mutal mutal uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatai iṅku ārāyvōm. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mutaṟpakutiyil, tamiḻnāṭṭil vantu vāḻntirunta ittāli tēcattavarāṉa peski eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar tām tamiḻil mutaṉmutal urainaṭainūl iyaṟṟiṉavar eṉpatu perum pāṉmai yōr karuttu. perumpāṉmaiyōr karuttu eṉṟālum, itu tavaṟāṉa muṭipākum. vīramāmuṉivarukku muṉṉarē, patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tamiḻ nāṭṭil vantu vāḻntirunta ittāliya tēcattavarāṉa irāparṭ nopili eṉṉum tattuvapōtaka cuvāmi pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷait tamiḻil iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. vīramāmuṉivaraippaṟṟit terintiruppatu pōla, tattuvapōtaka cuvāmiyaippaṟṟip palarukkut teriyātu. vīramāmuṉivarukku muṉṉarē ivar tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. eṉṟālum, ivarait “tamiḻ urainūliṉ tantai” eṉṟu colvataṟ killai. ēṉeṉṟāl, ivarukkum muṉṉarē, patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ uṇṭāyiruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ki.pi.1577ām āṇṭiltāṉ tamiḻil mutaṉ mutal urainaṭai nūl uṇṭāṉatākat terikiṟatu. inta nūlukkuk “kiṟittava vētōpatēcam”1 eṉpatu peyar. ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimārāl eḻuti acciṭappaṭṭa inta mutal urainaṭai nūl ippōtu intiyāvil illai eṉṟu terikiṟatu. oruvēḷai airōppāk kaṇṭattil uḷḷa puttakacālaikaḷiṟ kiṭaikkakkūṭum. inta nūlai yār iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu teriyavillai. cavēriyār eṉṉum ceyṉṭ sēviyar eḻutiṉār eṉṟu cilar collukiṟārkaḷ. itu tavaṟu. ēṉeṉṟāl, cavēriyārukkut tamiḻ eḻutap paṭikkat teriyātu. ākaiyāl, inta mutal tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlai avar iyaṟṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. inta nūl acciṭṭa piṟaku, 1579-il “kiṟittava vaṇakkam”2 eṉṉum nūl acciṭappaṭṭatu. inta urainaṭai nūlai eḻutiyavar āṉṟik3 eṉṉum ēcuviṉ capaiyaic cērnta pātiriyār eṉṟu terikiṟatēyaṉṟi, avaraippaṟṟiya vēṟu varalāṟu oṉṟum teriyavillai. inta nūlum namatu tēcattil ippōtu kiṭaikkavillai. pāris nakarap puttaka cālaiyil intap puttakattiṉ piratiyoṉṟu iruppatāka uṟutiyākat terikiṟatu.
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, mēṟcoṉṉa iraṇṭu nūlkaḷait tavira, ēcuviṉ capaiyaiccērnta airōppiyap pātirimār tamiḻ kaṟpataṟkākac cila nūlkaḷ acciṭap paṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, an nūlkaḷaip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyavillai. tamiḻil mutal urainaṭai nūl uṇṭāṉatu 1577-il eṉpatu maṭṭum uṟutiyākat terikiṟatu.
16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku, mēlē colliyapaṭi  17-ām  nūṟṟāṇṭil, rāpaṭ nopili eṉṉum tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār. inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ivarait tavira, aruḷāṉantar eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa jāṉ-ṭi-piriṭṭō pātiriyār cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷait tamiḻil eḻutiyiruppatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl aruḷāṉantacuvāmi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ emakku ituvarai kiṭaikkavillai. tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷaip paṟṟi maṟṟōriṭattil avar varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm.
ataṉ piṟaku,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, peski eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiyirukkiṟār. ivar kālattilē, tañcaik kaṭutta taraṅkam pāṭiyil vāḻntirunta cīkaṉ pālku mutalāṉa cermaṉi tēcattup pātirimārkaḷum cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. itē nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ tiruvāvaṭutuṟai ātīṉattaic cērnta mātavac civañāṉa cuvāmiyum cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiṉār. civañāṉa cuvāmiyiṉ urainaṭai vīramāmuṉivariṉ urai naṭaiyaip pōṉṟu mikacciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. cermaṉi tēcattavarāṉa cīkaṉ pālku aiyar eḻutiya urainaṭai potumakkaḷukku ēṟṟa koccaittamiḻnaṭaiyil amaintatu. vīramāmuṉivar, cīkaṉpālku aiyar ivarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟiṉai maṟṟōr iṭattiṟ kāṇka.
tamiḻurainaṭainūl mutalmutal tōṉṟiyatu 16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eṉṟālum, atu vērūṉṟi nilai peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ. 17,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻ urai naṭai nūlkaḷ atikamāka ēṟpaṭa villai. 1577-il mutal urainaṭai nūl uṇṭāṉataṟkup piṟaku, irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ varaiyilum, tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷarcci peṟāmalē kuṉṟiyiruntatu. ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka veḷippaṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. itu viyapputtāṉ. irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka urainaṭai vaḷarcci taṭaippaṭṭiruntu, piṟaku tiṭīreṉṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyateṉṟāl, taṭaippaṭṭiruntataṟkum, piṟaku tiṭīreṉṟu vaḷarntataṟkum kāraṇamirukkavēṇṭumaṉṟō? akkāraṇantāṉ yātu? kāraṇam araciyaṟ kuḻappaṅkaḷē. 16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil, namatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟaip puraṭṭip pārttāl itu naṉku viḷaṅkum, paṇṭaiya tamiḻ maṉṉarum ciṟṟaracarum aracāṭci iḻantu, putiya putiya aracarkaḷum talaivarkaḷum innāṭṭil tōṉṟiṉar. piṟaku avarum aḻintoḻintu, kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷum pāḷaiyakkārarkaḷum aracāṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kālattil valiyavaṉ meliyavaṉai aṭittup piṭuṅkuvatu eṉkiṟa muṟai nāṭeṅkum naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu. pireñcu, iṅkiliṣ kampeṉikkāra caṇṭaikaḷum, karnāṭaka navāppukaḷiṉ pōrkaḷum, pāḷaiyakkārariṉ tollaikaḷum, marāṭṭiyariṉ paṭaiyeṭuppum, aitarāli tippu cultāṉ kalakaṅkaḷum ākiya araciyaṟkuḻappaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, nāṭṭumakkaḷaip paṭātapāṭu paṭuttiviṭṭaṉa. nilai peṟṟa aracāṭciyum, uyirukkum poruḷukkum pātukāppum illā maiyālum, mēṟcoṉṉa kuḻappaṅkaḷiṉ kāraṇattālum, makkaḷ kalviyiṟ karuttuc celuttiṉārkaḷillai. āṉālum, kāriruṭṭil miṉmiṉi tōṉṟuvatu pōl, intak kuḻappak kālattilum camayat toṇṭāṟṟi vanta kiṟittuvap pātirikaḷum. caiva maṭātipatikaḷumē oruvāṟu kalviyiṟ karuttaic celutta muṭintatu. icciṟupāṉmai yōrākiya camaya ūḻiyarkaḷilum oru cilar tām urainaṭainūl iyaṟṟa muṉvantaṉar. viralviṭṭeṇṇak kūṭiyapaṭi mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa nālaintu urainaṭai nūlāciriyarkaḷait tavira, vēṟu āciriyarkaḷ 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai ēṟpaṭavillai.
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil araciyaṟ kuḻappaṅkaḷum caṇṭaikaḷum oḻintu, āṅkila aracāṅkam nilaipeṟṟu, uyirukkum poruḷukkum pātukāppum, tēcattil amaitiyum, oḻuṅkum ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, iyaṟkaiyākavē makkaḷ kalviyil karuttaic celuttiṉārkaḷ. urainaṭai nūlkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyai uṇarntu, urainaṭai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, innūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ pātiri mārkaḷālum aracāṅkat tārālum tēcameṅkum pāṭacālaikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭṭavarkaḷāl naṭattappaṭṭa pāṭacālaikaḷukku aracāṅkattār poruḷutaviceytu ūkkappaṭuttiṉar. ivvāṟu āṅkāṅkē pāṭacālaikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa paṭiyālum, ippāṭacālaikaḷil airōppiya muṟaippaṭi ilakkiyam, pūkōḷam, carittiram mutaliya ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷaiyum urainaṭaip pāṭamākak kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiyapaṭiyālum, tamiḻurainaṭainūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. iḵtaṉṟiyum, inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ accup poṟikaḷ nāṭu muḻutum āṅkāṅkē niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. aracāṅka aluvalkaḷil amarntirunta airōppiya uyartara aluvalāḷarkaḷum, camayat toṇṭāṟṟivanta airōppiya pātirimārum tamiḻmoḻiyaik kaṟkavēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam akkālattil ēṟpaṭṭiruntapaṭiyāl, avarkaḷ eḷitākat tamiḻaik kaṟkum poruṭṭu urainaṭai nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭu acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. “miṣaṉ” eṉṉum kiṟittuvac caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅkē niṟuvappaṭṭu, ac caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka matac cārpāṉa urainaṭai nūlkaḷ ērāḷamāka acciṭappaṭṭuk kuṟainta vilaikkuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṉṟiyum, ac caṅkaṅkaḷ urainaṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭa tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷai acciṭṭu vilaiyiṉṟi makkaḷukku vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. itaṉ vāyilākavum urainaṭainūlkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ parava vaḻiyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. pātirimārkaḷ ceytuvarum ittakaiya camaya ūḻiyattaikkaṇṭu, itukāṟum ikkāriyattil uṟaṅkikkiṭanta intukkaḷ viḻippaṭaintu, intu mataccārpāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum purāṇa itikācaṅkaḷaiyum urainaṭai nūlkaḷāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil, kiṟittuva matattārukkum, intu matattārukkum, caivarukkum vaiṇavarukkum, mataccārpāṉa vaḻakku etirvaḻakkukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa paṭiyāl, avaravarkaḷ tattam matattaip pukaḻntum ayalār matattaik kuṟai kūṟiyum, urainaṭaip puttakaṅkaḷaiyum, tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷaiyum eḻuti acciṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantaṉar.
ivai oru puṟamirukka, 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kalvi 
vaḷarcciyai muṉṉiṭṭuc cila caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, tamiḻ uṭainaṭai nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭa utavipurintaṉa. `ceṉṉaik kalvic caṅkam’4 eṉṉum oru capai ēṟpaṭṭu, pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷaiyum maṟṟum cila urainaṭaip puttakaṅkaḷaiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭatu. ‘ceṉṉaip pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkam’5 eṉṉum caṅkam 1850-il ēṟpaṭṭup pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷai eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭu utaviṟṟu. intac capaiyōr nalla urainaṭai nūl eḻutuvōrukkup poruḷ utaviceytu, paricaḷittu vantaṉar. ‘irāpiṉcaṉ kurūcō,’ ‘intiya carittiram,’ ‘ulaka carittiram’ mutaliya ciṟanta moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷ iccaṅkattār aḷitta paricu kāraṇamāka veḷi vantavai. akkālattil veḷivanta ciṟanta urainaṭai nūlkaḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum acciṭap paṭāmaiyāl ippōtu maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil aracāṇṭa kiḻakkintiya aracāṅkattār, ‘naṉṉūl’ ilakkaṇattai urainaṭaiyāka eḻutuvōrukku vekumati yaḷippatāka viḷamparam ceytārkaḷ. ataṉ payaṉākattāṉ tamiḻil urainaṭai ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ivaiyaṉṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ ceytit tāḷkaḷum6 māta vāra veḷiyīṭukaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil tōṉṟi ulāvat toṭaṅkiṉa. ivvāṟu paṟpala vaḻikaḷil tamiḻ urainaṭai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ nāṭṭil vērūṉṟic ceḻittōṅki vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṉa.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
