3. accup puttaka varalāṟu
iṉṟaiya ulakam muṉṉēṟṟamaṭaintu, nākarikam peṟṟuc ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppavaikaḷuḷ accup puttakamum oṉṟākum. kākitamum accup poṟiyum ēṟpaṭṭu, accup puttakam uṇṭāṉa piṟaku tāṉ, kalvi eṉṉum aṟivuoḷi nāṭeṅkum parava vaḻiyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē paṉai ēṭu, pataṉiṭṭa tōl mutaliya poruḷkaḷil makkaḷ nūlkaḷai eḻuti vantaṉar. ōlai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ puttakam eḻutuvatu kaṭiṉamāṉa kāriyam. oru puttakam eḻuti muṭippataṟkut tēka uḻaippu orupuṟa mirukka, kālattaiyum poruḷaiyum atikamākac celavu ceyya vēṇṭi yiruntatu. ākaiyāl, paṇṭaik kālattil, poruḷ uḷḷavar maṭṭum puttakam peṟṟu aṟivai aṭaiya vacatiyiruntatu; ēṉaiya peruntokaiyarāṉa ēḻai makkaḷ puttakam vāṅka vacatiyillāmalē yiruntaṉar. ik kāraṇattiṉāl, accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē, kalvi eṉṉum cuṭaroḷi, puttakam peṟa vacatiyuḷḷa ciṟupāṉmai yōriṭattil,--mikamikak kuṟaivāṉa oru cilariṭattil maṭṭum, miṉmiṉipōla oḷiviṭṭuk koṇṭirunta tēyaṉṟi, nāṭṭi luḷḷa ellā makkaḷiṭattilum avvaṟivoḷi parava vacatiyillāmalē iruntatu. accuppoṟi vanta piṟaku, kuṟainta celavil, ciṟu uḻaippil, kuṟukiya nērattil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭuvatāl, tōṭṭi mutal toṇṭaimāṉ varaiyil, nāṭṭumakkaḷ aṉaivarum puttakaṅkaḷaik kuṟainta vilaikku vāṅkippaṭittu, aṟivaippeṟa utaviyāyirukkiṟatu. tēca makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattiṟ paṭintirunta kallāmai eṉṉum kāriruṭ paṭalam, accup puttakam vanta piṟaku, cūriyaṉaik kaṇṭa paṉipōl maṟaiyat toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. potumakkaḷukkum puttakam vāṅka vacati ēṟpaṭṭa piṟakutāṉ, kalvi kaṟṟu mēṉmēlum aṟivaip perukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum ācai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. accup puttakam vantataṉāl potuvāka ulakattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutal ituvē.
accup puttakam varuvataṟku muṉṉē, nam nāṭṭup potu makkaḷum nūlkaḷaip paṭittu aṟivaip perukka vacatiyillāmal iruntaṉar. oru ciṟu tokaiyiṉarākiya paṭitta kūṭṭattār paṉai yōlaikaḷil nūlkaḷai eḻuti vantaṉar. iruppeḻuttāṇi koṇṭu paṉaiyōlaikaḷil nūlkaḷai eḻutimuṭikkat tēka uḻaippum, kālac celavum poruṭcelavum atikamāka ēṟpaṭṭatu orupakkamirukka, avaṟṟaip paṭippatum kaṭiṉamākaviruntatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, cuvaṭikaḷai vacatiyākak kaiyāḷa muṭiyāta varuttattōṭu paṭikka muyaṉṟālum, ēṭukaḷil mūlam iṉṉatu, urai iṉṉatu eṉpatu teriyāmalum, puḷḷi peṟavēṇṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟāmalum orē eḻuttu mayamākak kāṇappaṭum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka kaḷvar eṉṟu ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟu vaittuk koḷvōm. itaik kaḷavā eṉṟu paṭippatā kaḷvar eṉṟu paṭippatā eṉkiṟa aiyam viraivil tīrātu. ivvitam ēṭukaḷil meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷi peṟātiruppatiṉāl uṇṭākum aiyappāṭukaḷ orupuṟamirukka muṟṟuppuḷḷi, kāṟpuḷḷi, araip puḷḷikaḷum kāṇappaṭā. ivaṟṟai ellāmviṭa mikavum varuttan tarum vēṟoṉṟu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷiṟ pala vēṟu pāṭukaḷum iṭaiccerukalkaḷum kāṇappaṭuvatu tāṉ. accup puttaṅkaḷilō iṭaiccerukalum pāṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷum nuḻaivataṟ kiṭamillai. nuḻaikkavum muṭiyātu. ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷil ivai ilēcāka nuḻaivataṟku iṭamuṇṭu. eppaṭi eṉṟāl, ōr āciriyaṉ oru nūlai eḻutiṉāṉeṉṟāl, annūlai maṟṟavarkaḷ paṭi eḻutikkoḷvatu paṇṭaiya vaḻakkam. ippaṭip paṭikaḷ eḻutumpōtu, avaṟṟai eḻutuvōrkkut terintō teriyā malō pāṭavēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭuvatum uṇṭu. pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷiṉāl nulāciriyaṉ karuttukku māṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷum annūlūṭ pukuntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṭeḻutuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmaiyiṉālum, koḷkai vēṟupāṭṭiṉālum maṟṟum piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum, pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruppatai ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiltāṉ kāṇamuṭiyum. ippoḻutu tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kamparāmāyaṇa ēṭṭuppaṭikaḷaik koṇṭu vantu avaṟṟai oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oppiṭṭup pārppōmāṉāl, ovvoru cuvaṭiyilum vevvēṟu pāṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷ iruppataik kāṇalām. ivvakaiyē maṟṟa nūṟcuvaṭikaḷaiyum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu oppiṭṭup pārppōmāṉāl, avaṟṟilum pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷaiyāvatu iṭaiccerukal kaḷaiyāvatu kāṇamuṭiyum. ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷ ellām orē pāṭa muḷḷavaiyā yiruppatu arumai. tirukkuṟaḷukku uraiyeḻutiya maṇakkuṭavar, tirukkuṟaḷ 7-ām atikārattiṟku “makkaṭpēṟu” eṉṉum talaippeyar koṭuttirukka, maṟṟoru uraiyāciriyarākiya parimēlaḻakar atē atikārattiṟkup “putalvaraip peṟutal” eṉṉum talaippeyar iṭṭiruppatu yāvarum aṟintatē. iḵtu, āṇmakkaḷukkum peṇ makkaḷukkum cama urimaiyuṇṭu eṉṉum maṇakkuṭavar karuttaiyum, peṇmakkaḷukku āṇmakkaḷaippōl cama urimai kūṭātu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar koḷkaiyaiyum naṉku viḷakkuvatōṭu, ēṭeḻutuvōr allatu uraiyiṭuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmaikkum matak koḷkaikkum ēṟpa nūlkaḷiṟ pāṭavēṟupāṭu amaikkappaṭum eṉpataṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟākavum niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvakai ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiṟ pala kuṟaipāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvakai kuṟaipāṭukaḷum iṭarppāṭukaḷum accup puttakattiṟ kāṇappaṭā. niṟka.
iṉi, tamiḻil accup puttakam vanta varalāṟṟiṉai ārāyvōm. namatu nāṭṭil airōppiyar vanta piṟaku tāṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. namatu nāṭṭil kiṟittuva matattaip paravacceyvataṟkākap perum toṇṭāṟṟi vanta ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimārkaḷ mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil accupputtakam uṇṭāk kiṉārkaḷ. intiya moḻikaḷil mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻmoḻiyilētāṉ. piṟakutāṉ ēṉaiya intiya moḻikaḷil accup puttakam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
namatu nāṭṭil tamiḻ accup puttakam uṇṭāṉa varalāṟṟai ārāyvataṟku muṉṉam, airōppāk kaṇṭattil acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatu amaivuṭaittu. āṉāl, airōppāk kaṇṭattil acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅ kaḷiṉ muḻu varalāṟu ippōtu emakkuk kiṭaikkavillai. eṉiṉum, yāmaṟinta varaiyil eḻutuvōm. malaiyāḷattut tāvara  nūl eṉṉum puttakam malaiyāḷa tēcattil uḷḷa koṭi ceṭikaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟum nūl. inta nūlil marañceṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ maṭṭum tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. innūl ollāntu tēcattiṉ1 talainakarāṉa āmsṭarṭām2 nakarattil 1686-l acciṭappaṭṭatu. innūlil marañceṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai maṭṭum kuṟippiṭum tamiḻ acceḻuttu annakarattil uṇṭākkap paṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. ataṉpiṟaku jermaṉi tēcattaic cērnta hāli3 nakarattil,1780-ām āṇṭil, (tañcait taraṅkampāṭiyil irunta cīkaṉpālku aiyar eṉṉum ṭēṉiṣ miṣaṉ talaivariṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi) tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaku, anta nakarattiṟ cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṟ patikkappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. ṣūls aiyar eṉṉum cermāṉiyar “paratīs tōṭṭam”4 eṉṉum nūlait tamiḻil moḻi peyarttu 1749-il acciṟ patippittār. “uṇmaik kiṟittuvam”5 eṉṉum puttakattai “ñāṉak kaṇṇāṭi” eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tamiḻil moḻipeyartteḻuti 1750-il acciṭṭār. ip puttakaṅkaḷ hāli nakarattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. (ṣūls aiyar tañcaikkaṭutta taraṅkampāṭiyil vantu tamiḻ kaṟṟa pātiriyāvar.) iṉṉum cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ cermaṉi tēcattil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil acciṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ ippōtu emakkuk kiṭaikka villaiyākaiyāl, avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟṟai eḻuta iyalavillai. cermaṉi tēcattil tamiḻpputtakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭa atē kālattilō, ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉ piṉṉākavō, ittāli tēcattaic cērnta urōmā puriyilum tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷil, kiṟittuva matattaip paravacceyyum poruṭṭu, kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ kiṟittuva mata nūlkaḷai acciṭuvataṟkāka accuppoṟi nilaiyam urōmā puriyil amaikkappaṭṭirunta ceyti airōppiyar  eḻutiyuḷḷa paḻaiya nūlkaḷiṉāl veḷippaṭukiṟatu. anta accup poṟi nilaiyattil intiya moḻikaḷiṟ cila nūlkaḷ acciṭap paṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl aṅku acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻpputta kaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ ippōtu aṟiyak kūṭāmalirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, namatu nāṭṭil mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam vanta varalāṟṟiṉai ārāyvōm. intiyāvilē, atilum tamiḻ moḻiyil, mutaṉ mutal accup puttakam uṇṭākkiya perumai ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimāraic cērntatu eṉṟu mēlē coṉ ṉōm. tamiḻ accup puttakavaralāṟṟaippaṟṟit tiṭṭamākak kūṟum nūl oṉṟēṉum illai. ākaiyāl, āṅkāṅkuc citaṟikkiṭakkum ceytikaḷiliruntum kuṟippukaḷiliruntum ivvaralāṟu oruvāṟu eḻutappaṭukiṟatu.
malaiyāḷa tēcattil uḷḷa kocciyilum, ataṉ piṟaku cumār 1578-l, tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa puṉṉaikkāyal eṉṉum iṭattilum, ēcuviṉ capai pātirimār6 accup poṟikaḷai ēṟpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. piṟaku vaippukkōṭṭai, ampalakkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum accuppoṟikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. inta accup poṟikaḷ yāvum16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyilē tāṉ amaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
mutal accupputtakam ki.pi.1577-l acciṭappaṭṭatu. “kiṟittuva   vētōpatēcam”7 eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa apputtakam vaippuk kōṭṭai eṉṉum ūril acciṭappaṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. jōṇ kōṇsālvas eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa, speyṉ tēcattavarākiya ēcuviṉ capaip pātiriyār uṇṭākkiya acceḻuttukkaḷaik koṇṭu ipputtakam acciṭappaṭṭatu. aṭuttapaṭiyāka,1579-l “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam”8 eṉṉum puttakam kocciyil acciṟ patikkappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil pōrccukīs tamiḻp puttakam9 oṉṟum malaiyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu. iḵtu iṉṉāci āccāmaṇi10 eṉṉum cutēci oruvar uṇṭākkiya acceḻuttuk koṇṭu acciṭap paṭṭatu. aṉṟiyum, airōppiyap pātirimārkaḷ tamiḻ kaṟṟuk koḷḷuvataṟku utaviyākac cila puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa vākat terikiṉṟaṉa. mēṟ collappaṭṭa “kiṟittava vētōpatēcam,”  “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam”  eṉṉum iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷaip paṟṟic cilarukku ōr aiyappāṭu uṇṭu. aḵteṉṉaveṉṟāl, ivviru puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻmoḻiyil acciṭappaṭṭaṉavā, allatu malaiyāḷa moḻiyil acciṭap paṭṭaṉavā eṉkiṟa aiyappāṭutāṉ. avai malaiyāḷa moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭavai eṉṟu cilarum, tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭavai eṉṟu vēṟu cilarum karutukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, ap puttakaṅkaḷ ippōtu intiyāviṟ kiṭaiyā. mēṟpaṭip puttakaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi eḻutiya airōppiyarkaḷ avai “malapār” moḻiyil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. “malapār” moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭavai eṉpataṉālum, malaiyāḷa tēcattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭataṉālum, avai malaiyāḷap puttakaṅ kaḷākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu oru kaṭciyār koḷkai. ivarkaḷ niṉaippatu cariyāṉateṉṟu tōṉṟalām. āṉāl, uḷ nuḻaintu ārāyntu pārttāl, avarkaḷiṉ karuttut tavaṟāṉa teṉṟum, uṇmai avar karuttukku māṟāṉateṉṟum teriyavarum. malaiyāḷa tēcattilē acciṭṭa paṭiyiṉālē apputtakaṅkaḷ malaiyāḷa moḻiyiltāṉ irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉaippatu tavaṟu. “malapār” moḻi eṉṟāl malaiyāḷa moḻi eṉṟu karutuvatum tavaṟākum. ippoḻutu “malapār”  eṉkiṟa collukkup poruḷ vēṟu; cumār nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ic collukkirunta poruḷ vēṟu. intak kālattil “malapār” eṉṉum airōppiyac col malaiyāḷa tēcattaik kuṟikkiṟatu. āṉāl, itē “malapār” eṉṉum col cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻar allatu tamiḻ moḻi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅki vantatēyaṉṟi, ippōtu vaḻaṅkum poruḷaik kuṟippiṭavillai. namatu nāṭṭil vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu vantirunta palvēṟu airōppiya iṉattārum tamiḻarai “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟum, tamiḻmoḻiyai “malapār moḻi” eṉṟum akkālattil vaḻaṅki vantatai avarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkum nūlkaḷiliruntu ippōtum terintukoḷḷalām. ivar maṭṭumiṉṟi, ilaṅkaiyai āṇṭirunta ollāntar mutaliya airōppiyarum ilaṅkait tamiḻarai “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟu vaḻaṅkivantaṉar eṉpataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ pala nūlkaḷiliruntu kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkālattil ip peyar palarukku viyappaiyuṇṭākkak kūṭum. āṉāl iḵtu uṇmai eṉpatu ārāyntu pārttāl viḷaṅkum.
airōppiyar tamiḻarai “malapārikaḷ” eṉṟu ēṉ vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ? ippeyarai avarkaḷ mukammatiyariṭattiliruntu kaṟṟatākat terikiṟatu. airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉamē, mukammatiyar cilar teṉ intiyāvukku vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu vaḻi naṭaiyaippaṟṟi eḻutiya puttakaṅkaḷil teṉ intiyāvai “māpār” eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppatākavum, piṟkālattil vanta airōppiyar “māpār” eṉṉum collai “malapār” eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. teṉ intiyāvaik kuṟippiṭum “māpār” eṉṉum collai “malapār” ākki olitta airōppiyar teṉ intiyāvil vāḻum makkaḷaiyum “malapār” eṉṟu vaḻaṅki ṉārkaḷ. piṟaku iccol, teṉ intiyāvil peruṅkuṭimakkaḷāka virukkum tamiḻarukkum, avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyākiya tamiḻukkum amaintiruntatu. ippōtu accol paṇṭaiya poruḷkaḷai utaṟiviṭṭu, malaiyāḷa tēcattaimaṭṭum kuṟikkiṟatu. “malapār” eṉṉum iccolliṉ varalāṟṟiṉai aṟiyātavar, ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkum poruḷil accollai vaittu ārāyvatāl, uṇmai pulappaṭāmal iṭarppaṭukiṟārkaḷ. inta iṭarppāṭṭiṉāltāṉ mēṟcoṉṉa iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum malaiyāḷa moḻip puttakaṅka ḷeṉṟu avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ karuttukkut tuṇaiyāka, ap puttakaṅkaḷ malaiyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭṭatai avarkaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. malaiyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭum puttakam malaiyāḷa moḻiyiltāṉ irukkavēṇṭum eṉṉum kaṭṭāyam illai. kiṟittuva matattaip paravac ceyta ēcuviṉ capaip pātirikaḷiṉ talaimai iṭam ak kālattil malaiyāḷa tēcattil iruntatu. ēcuviṉ capaip pātirikaḷiṉ talaivarum malaiyāḷa tēcattiltāṉ vāḻntu vantār. ākaiyāl, matac cārpāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭuvataṟkuriya accup poṟikaḷum avviṭattilēyē ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil maturaiyil vāḻntirunta tattuva pōtaka cuvāmi eṉṉum ēcuviṉ capaip pātiriyār tamiḻil eḻutiya “ñāṉōpatēca kāṇṭam” eṉṉum puttakam, tamiḻnāṭṭil akkālattil accup poṟi illātapaṭiyāl, malaiyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, pātirimārāl niṟuvappaṭṭirunta accuppoṟi nilaiyattil acciṭappaṭṭateṉṟum apputtakattai acciṟpatippippataṟkākat tamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta antrēppirayā eṉṉum pātiriyār ampalakkāṭṭukkuc ceṉṟār eṉṟum varalāṟṟu vāyilāy aṟikiṟōm. itaṉāl, malaiyāḷak karaiyil tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum.
ituvaṉṟiyum, mēṟkuṟitta “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” “kiṟittuva vētōpatēcam” eṉṉum iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻp putta kaṅkaḷtām eṉpataṟkuvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avai eṉṉa veṉṟāl, taraṅkampāṭic tiruccapaiyaic cērnta sārṭṭōriyas pātiriyār11 ceṉṉaikku vaṭakkēyuḷḷa paḻavēṟkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, oru kiṟittuva matapōtakar 1579-il kocciyil acciṭappaṭṭa ‘kiṟittuva vaṇakkam’ eṉṉum tamiḻ accup puttakattaip pārtteḻutappaṭṭa kaiyeḻuttuppaṭi oṉṟai vaittiruntatait tām pārttatāka,1743-il eḻutiyirukkiṟār. mēlum, antap pātiriyār kīḻ varum ceytiyaiyum eḻuti yirukkiṟār; kocciyil “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” acciṭappaṭṭa atē accukkūṭattil apputtakam acciṭappaṭṭa atē āṇṭil, ēcuviṉ capaip pātiriyārāṉa mārkkas jārj12 eṉpavarāl eḻutap paṭṭu, mēṟpaṭi capaiyaic cērnta maṟṟoru pātiriyārāṉa āṉriks13 eṉpavarāl tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakattait taraṅkampāṭiyil tām kaṇṭatāka eḻutiyirukkiṟār. itaṉāl anta iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷum tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷē eṉpatu aiyamaṟa viḷaṅkum.
“kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum accup puttakam tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭatu tāṉ eṉpataṟku iṉṉoru cāṉṟum uṇṭu. ṭākṭar pi.jē. tōmas em.ē.,eṉpavar “kēraḷattilē kiṟittīya cākittiyam” eṉṉum puttakattai malaiyāḷattil eḻutiyirukkiṟār. avar ap puttakattil “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakattaip paṟṟi eḻutiyiruppatu:- “pāris nakarattil pēr peṟṟa sōrpōṉ”14 carva kalācālaiyaic cērnta puttaka cālaiyil ki.pi.1579-il, malaiyāḷa tēcattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭa oru vētōpatēca puttakam iruppatāka aṟintēṉ. uṭaṉē, apputtakattiṉ oṉṟiraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷaip pōṭṭōp piṭittu aṉuppumpaṭi pātar hōsṟṟaṉ eṉpavar mūlamāka ēṟpāṭu ceytēṉ. appaṭam vantu cērntatum ataip pārttapōtu, apputtakam tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷāl acciṭappaṭṭateṉṟu aṟintēṉ. cēra maṇṭalattilum cōḻa maṇṭalattilum upayōkappaṭum poruṭṭu accaṭikkappaṭṭatāka atil eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu koṇṭu, akkālattil iṅku (malaiyāḷa tēcattil) tamiḻ moḻikku mutaṉmaiyaḷikkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu” eṉpatu. itiliruntu 1579-il malaiyāḷa tēcattil acciṭappaṭṭa “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakam tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭa teṉpatum, ataṉ paṭi pāris nakarattil irukkiṟa teṉpatum, viḷaṅkukiṟatu, yām aṟinta varaiyil, patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil (1550 mutal 1600 varaiyil) acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ “kiṟittuva vētōpa tēcam,” “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum iraṇṭu nūlkaḷtām. vēṟu cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭirukkalām, āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ vivaram teriya villai.
paṟaṅkiyar kālattil ēcuviṉ capaip pātirikaḷiṉ mēṟ pārvaiyil, teṉ intiyāvil kīḻkkaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷil accup poṟikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. ivaitām mutaṉmutal intiyāvil niṟuvappaṭṭa accup poṟikaḷ.
malaiyāḷa tēcattil ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil  1550-il  oru accup poṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu.
tirunelvēliyaic cērnta puṉṉaik kāyal eṉṉum iṭattil, 1578-il, ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimār tamiḻ kaṟpataṟkāka oru kallūri niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ik kallūriyiṉ talaimai āciriyarāka viruntavar eṉrīkes15 eṉpavar. intak kallūriyiṉ mēṟpārvaiyil mēṟpaṭi āṇṭil oru accup poṟi amaikkappaṭṭatu. vārīyā16 eṉpavar iṅku accu eḻuttukkaḷai uṇṭākkic cila tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷai acciṟ patippittār. iṅku acciṭappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷ iṉṉavai eṉṟu teriyavillai. ivar uṇṭākkiya accu eḻuttu mara acceḻuttāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. 1578-il “kiṟittuva vētōpatēcam”, “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakaṅkaḷ ivviṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kocciyil, 1577-il ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimār oru accup poṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ. aṅku acciṭap paṭṭa tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷiṉ vivaram teriyavillai.
kōvāp paṭṭiṉattil, 1550-il, pōrccukal tēcattiliruntu iraṇṭu accup poṟikaḷ vantaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivai upayōkappaṭuttappaṭāmalē yiruntaṉa. yām aṟinta varaiyil, 1557-il tāṉ mutaṉ mutal kōvāppaṭṭiṉattil puttakam acciṭappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, apputtakam tamiḻmoḻiyil acciṭap paṭṭataṉṟu. iṅkut tamiḻ puttakaṅkaḷ eppōtāvatu acciṭap paṭṭaṉavā eṉpatum teriyavillai.
malaiyāḷa tēcattil cēnta maṅkalattaic cērnta aṟuppuk kōṭṭai eṉṉum iṭattil, 1557-il oru tēvālayamum, pātiri kaḷiṉ maṭamum, oru accup poṟiyum niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. kocciyilum itē āṇṭiltāṉ accuppoṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ivviṭattil kōṇsālvas eṉṉum pātiriyār tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷai uṇṭākki, “kiṟittuva vaṇakkam” eṉṉum puttakattai acciṭṭatākac collukiṟārkaḷ. intap puttakam tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa puṉṉaikkāyal accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatāka vēṟu cilar eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉ uṇmai viḷaṅkavillai. ivviṭattil tamiḻmoḻiyilum cila nūlkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vivaram teriyavillai.
malaiyāḷa tēcattil, ampalakkāṭu eṉṉum iṭattil, maṟṟoru poṟi 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil amaikkap paṭṭatu. enta āṇṭil niṟuvappaṭṭateṉṟu teriyavillai. āṉāl, 1679-il iṉṉāci āccāmaṇi17 eṉṉum upatēciyār tamiḻil acceḻuttuk kaḷai uṇṭākkiṉār. mēṟpaṭi āṇṭilē antōṉi ṭipril pātiriyār eḻutiya tamiḻ-pōrccukīs akarātiyum, tekōsṟṟā pātiriyār eḻutiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇamum iṅku acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ muḻu vivaramum kiṭaikka villai.
paṟaṅkiyar ilaṅkait tīvilum celvākkup peṟṟirun tārkaḷ. aṅku yāḻppāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷil palaraik kiṟittavarākkiyum, pala kōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiyum mata ūḻiyam ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ yāḻppāṇattil accup poṟikaḷ amaittu, tamiḻ nūlkaḷai acciṭṭārkaḷā eṉpatu teriyavillai. yām ārāyntavaraiyil illai eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. paṟaṅkiyarukkup piṟaku, intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyyavanta ollāntar, piṟkālattiṟ paṟaṅkiyarkaḷai intiyāviliruntum ilaṅkaiyiliruntum turatti viṭṭu, paṟaṅkiyariṉ āṭciyai aḻittu viṭṭārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, paṟaṅkikaḷiṉ cārpākac camaya ūḻiyam ceytirunta ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimārkaḷaiyum ūrai viṭṭup pōkumpaṭi kaṇṭippāṉa uttaravu iṭṭārkaḷ. ēcuviṉ capaip pātirimārkaḷ pōyviṭṭataṉāl avarkaḷ ceytuvanta accu vēlaikaḷum niṟuttappaṭṭaṉa. ollāntarum tamatu kiṟittuva matattaip parappuvataṟkāka muyaṟci ceytārkaḷ. āṉāl, intiyāvil avarkaḷ tamatu vēlaiyai atikamākac ceyya villai eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ollāntar intiyāvil tamiḻ nūlkaḷai acciṭṭatākat teriyavillai. ollāntar kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil accuvēlai niṉṟu viṭṭatu eṉṟu uṟutiyākac collalām. āṉāl, ilaṅkaiyil yāḻppāṇattilum, koḷumpilum kalācālaikaḷ amaittu, kirīkku, lattīṉ, ollānta pāṣai, tamiḻ mutaliya moḻikaḷaik kaṟpittu vantārkaḷeṉpatum, oru accup poṟiyum niṟuviṉārkaḷ eṉpatum terikiṉṟaṉa. yāḻppāṇattut tamiḻarākiya mellō pātiriyār18 ollāntaruṭaiya kallūriyiṟ kalvi kaṟṟut tērntavarkaḷil oruvar. inta mellō pātiriyār epirēyu, kirīk, ilattīṉ, pōrttukīciyam, ollāntu, tamiḻ eṉṉum āṟu moḻikaḷil vallavar. ivar kirēkka moḻiyiliruntu tamiḻil moḻi peyartta putiya ēṟpāṭu (viviliya vētam) 1749-l ollāntaruṭaiya accukkūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭatu. ilaṅkaiyil ollāntar kālattil acciṭappaṭṭa vēṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷ teriyavillai. āṉāl, mēlē colliyapaṭi ollāntar intiyāviliruntu paṟaṅkiyarait turattiviṭṭa piṟaku, kattōlikkak kiṟittavar puttakaṅkaḷai acciṭuvatu niṉṟu viṭṭatu.
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil tañcaikkaṭutta taraṅkampāṭiyilirunta ṭēṉiṣ miṣaṉariyāṉa sīkaṉpālku aiyar, tamiḻnāṭṭil accup poṟi illāta kuṟaiyaippaṟṟic cermaṉi tēcattiluḷḷa tam naṇparkaḷukku eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷiliruntum, ak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭil accuppoṟi oṉṟēṉum iruntirukka villai eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. sīkaṉpālku aiyar eḻutiya kaṭitattil, tamakku vēṇṭiya tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷaiyum, pāṭacālai māṇavarukku vēṇṭiya pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷaiyum, ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷil eḻutuvittu varuvataṉāl, atikappaṭiyāṉa kālac celavum, paṇac celavum ākiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, avvitam paṇamum kālamum atikamākac celaviṭṭālum cila paṭikaḷ tām eḻuti muṭikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, ākaiyāl paṇac celavaik kuṟaittut turitamākap puttakaṅkaḷai ērāḷamāka acciṭuvataṟku utaviyākat tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷaiyum accup poṟiyaiyum aṉuppi vaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tamatu cermaṉi tēcattu naṇparkaḷukku eḻutiyirukkiṟār. ivaratu vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅki, cermaṉi yiliruntu accup poṟiyum, tamiḻ acceḻuttukkaḷum aṉuppap paṭṭaṉa. ivai 1713-ām āṇṭu taraṅkampāṭikku vantu cērntaṉa. itu tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṉ mutal amaikkappaṭṭa accuppoṟi eṉpatil aiyamillai. itil pala tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. akkālattil acciṭuvataṟkuk kākitam airōppāviliruntu atikamāka varuvatillaiyākaiyāl, taraṅkampāṭic capaiyōr oru kākitap paṭṭarai ēṟpaṭuttik kākitam ceytaṉar. āṉāl uḻaippukkut takunta payaṉ kiṭaikkātapaṭiyāl, attoḻil ciṟitu kālattukkup piṟaku niṟuttappaṭṭatu. ituvē intiyāvil mutaṉ mutal ēṟpaṭṭa kākitat toḻiṟcālai eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. intat taraṅkampāṭi accup poṟi ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku potumakkaḷ accup puttakaṅkaḷai vāṅkip paṭikka atikamāka muṟpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu.
1761-m āṇṭil, iṅkilīṣkārar putuccēriyaip piṭittaṉar. appoḻutu āṅkilēyar pireñcuk kavarṉariṉ araṇmaṉaiyil irunta ōr accup poṟiyaik kaippaṟṟiṉar. kaippaṟṟiya antap poṟiyaic ceṉṉaikkuk koṇṭuvantu, vēppēri eṉṉum iṭattil irunta miṣaṉarimāriṉ pārvaiyil viṭṭuvaittaṉar. itu tamiḻ nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭāvatu accup poṟiyākum. itil aracāṅkattārukku vēṇṭiya puttakaṅkaḷum miṣaṉari mārukku vēṇṭiya puttakaṅkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. taraṅkam pāṭic capaiyiṉ accukkūṭattiṉāl potu makkaḷukku vēṇṭiya accup puttakaṅkaḷ avvaḷavaiyum uṇṭākka muṭiyāmalirunta kuṟai ceṉṉai accup poṟi uṇṭāṉapiṟaku nīṅkiviṭṭatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil inta iraṇṭu accup poṟikaḷ tām iruntaṉa.
āṉāl, 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, miṣaṉarimār tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ capaikaḷai ēṟpaṭutti mummuramāka camayattoṇṭu ceyya muṟpaṭṭārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum, muntiya nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷai viṭa inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ērāḷamāṉa pāṭacālaikaḷai ēṟpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. intukkaḷum mukammatiyaruṅkūṭat taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ matattaip parappuvataṟkākap puttakaṅkaḷaip putiyaṉa putiyaṉavāka acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiṉatum inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ. aṉṟiyum, ceytikaḷaik kūṟum nāḷtāḷ, kiḻamait tāḷkaḷum inta nūṟṟāṇṭilē tāṉ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. iḵtaṉṟiyum, intiyar accup poṟi vaikkak kūṭāteṉṟirunta taṭai 1835-ām āṇṭil nīkkappaṭṭatu. atumutal tamiḻarum accup poṟikaḷ amaikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl inta nūṟṟāṇṭil accup puttakaṅkaḷ mikutiyāka acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, kīḻkkaṇṭa accukkūṭaṅkaḷ miṣaṉarimārāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
“es.pi.si.kē.19 eṉṉum caṅkattār ceṉṉaiyil 1815-l oru accuppoṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ.
tirunelvēlip pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil oru matap piracāra caṅkam 1822-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭu, ataṉ cārpāka ōr accuppoṟi niṟuvappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil nākarkōyil eṉṉum iṭattilum oru capaiyum accuppoṟiyum niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa.
1830-l neyyūr eṉṉum iṭattil parappuk kaḻakamu(piracāra capai)m accup poṟiyum amaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
1821-l yāḻppāṇattu nallūril carcmicaṉ capaiyār ōr accup poṟi niṟuviṉārkaḷ.
1840-l putuccēriyil ōr accuppoṟi nilaiyam niṟuvappaṭṭatu.
1870-l ilaṅkaik kattōlik capaiyār ōr accup poṟiyaik koḻumpil ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṟaku, iḵtu yāḻppāṇattukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
mēṟcoṉṉa accuk kūṭaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷivanta accup puttakaṅkaḷ mikap pala. accupputtakaṅkaḷai uṇṭākkuvataṟku ivai nalla vēlai ceytuvantaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟi, “ceṉṉaik kalvic capai,”20 “ceṉṉaip pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkam,”21 “teṉṉintiyak kiṟittuvap pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkam”22 mutaliya caṅkaṅkaḷum accup puttakaṅkaḷai veḷippaṭuttuvatil 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē atikamāka uḻaittu vantaṉa.

4. viññāṉa nūl varalāṟu
airōppiyariṉ toṭarpiṉāl tamiḻmoḻiyil urainaṭai nūlkaḷum accupputtakaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭatu pōlavē, maṟṟoru vakai nūlum uṇṭāyirukkiṟatu. atutāṉ viññāṉanūl. nām viññāṉa nūl eṉṟu colluvatu pūkōḷa nūl, vāṉa nūl, pirāṇi nūl, kṣēttirak kaṇitam, tēcacarittiram, uṭal nūl mutaliyavai kaḷaiyē. airōppiyar varuvataṟku muṉṉē inta viññāṉa nūlkaḷ (vāṉa nūlait tavira) tamiḻ moḻiyilum ēṉaiya intiya moḻikaḷilum iruntaṉavillai. evaiyēṉum iruntaṉaveṉṟāl. avai uṇmaikku māṟāṉa kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷākattāṉ iruntirukkakkūṭum. pāl, tayir, ney, karuppañcāṟu mutalāṉa eḻuvakaip poruḷkaḷāl nirampappeṟṟa ēḻukaṭalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, cakaraṉuṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷ tōṇṭiyapaṭiyāl cākaram (kaṭal) uṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatum, malaikaḷ paṇṭaik kālattiṟ ciṟakukaḷaip peṟṟu ākāyattil iṅkum aṅkum viruppampōl paṟantu tirintu koṇṭiruntapōtu avaṟṟiṉ ciṟakukaḷai intiraṉ veṭṭi vīḻttiyapaṭiyāl avai paṟakkamuṭiyāmal pūmiyilē viḻuntu nakara muṭiyāmal aṅkaṅkē kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, pūmiyaip pāypōl curuṭṭik koṇṭupōy ōr acuraṉ kaṭalukkuḷ oḷittu vaittāṉ eṉpatum, ivai pōṉṟa kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷumē nammuṭaiya paṇṭaip pūkōḷa nūlkaḷ. pūmiyaic cūriyaṉ cuṟṟi varukiṟa teṉpatum, irāku kētu eṉṉum pāmpukaḷ cūriya cantirarkaḷai oru pakai kāraṇamāka viḻuṅkuvatāl cūriya cantira kiraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, takkaṉuṭaiya cāpattiṉāl cantiraṉ tēyvatum civaṉuṭaiya aruḷiṉāl atu vaḷarvatum nēriṭukiṟa teṉpatum, ivai pōṉṟavaikaḷumē namatu paṇṭaikkāla vāṉa cāttiraṅkaḷ. mēkam kaṭalil mēyntu taṇṇīraik kuṭittu vāṉattiṟ ceṉṟu maḻaiyākap peykiṟa teṉpatum, intiraṉuṭaiya kōpat tiṉāl iṭi miṉṉalkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, ivai pōṉṟa kaṟpaṉaikaḷumē paṇṭaikkāla iyaṟkaic cāttiraṅkaḷ. ittakaiya kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷaip paṭittum kēṭṭum paṇṭaik kāla makkaḷ uṇmaiyuṇarāmal mūṭattaṉattil āḻntiruntaṉar. airōppiyariṉ toṭarpu uṇṭāṉa piṟaku, pātirimārkaḷ pāṭacālaikaḷai amaittu, namatu nāṭṭuc ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷukku airōppiya vaḻakkattaip piṉpaṟṟip pāṭaṅkaḷaip pōtikka muṟpaṭṭapoḻutu, eḻutal, paṭittal, kaṇakkuppōṭutal eṉṉum mūṉṟuṭaṉ maṭṭum nillāmal, pūkōḷa nūl, vāṉa nūl, iyaṟkaip poruḷ nūl, kṣēttirak kaṇitam, tēcacarittiram mutaliya viññāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. pāṭacālaikaḷil ittakaiya viññāṉa nūlkaḷaip pōtikkat toṭaṅkiyapaṭiyāl, tamiḻil innūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mutalil pātiri mārum micaṉarimārum, piṉṉar aracāṅkattārum viññāṉa nūlkaḷait tamiḻil eḻuti acciṭṭum piṟarai eḻutumpaṭi tūṇṭiyum icceykaiyil pērutavi ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ.
inta viññāṉa nūlkaḷum ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilētāṉ tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. innūlkaḷiṉ muḻuvaralāṟṟiṉaiyum kūṟuvataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ muṟṟum emakkuk kiṭaikkavillai. kiṭaitta cilavaṟṟaik koṇṭu oruvāṟu kūṟa muṟpaṭukiṉṟōm.
“ceṉṉaip pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkam” eṉṉum ic caṅkam ēṟpaṭṭu, pāṭacālaip puttakaṅkaḷaiyum vēṟu puttakaṅkaḷaiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭu vantatu. iccaṅkam 1850-mutal atikac cuṟucuṟuppāka vēlaiceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iccaṅkam matac cārpaṟṟa potuvāṉa caṅkam. pāṭacālaip puttakaṅkaḷai acciṭṭu utaviyatōṭu pūkōḷam, carittiram mutaliya viññāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum tamiḻil eḻuti veḷippaṭuttiyatu. tamiḻil putiya nūlkaḷai eḻutuvōrukku naṉkoṭaiyaḷittu ūkkap paṭuttiyatu iccaṅkam. “intutēca carittiram,” “ulaka carittiram,” “pala tēca carittiram,” “ irāpiṉcaṉ kurūcō” mutalāṉa nūlkaḷ iccaṅkattiṉ ūkkattiṉāl veḷivantavai.
itait tavira, kiṟittuva matac cārpāka, “teṉṉintiyak kiṟittuvap pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkam”1 eṉṉum caṅkam 1854-l ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum, pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai, nākarkōyil, neyyūr, yāḻppāṇam mutaliya ūrkaḷilum kiṟittuva micaṉarimār, ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil accukkūṭaṅkaḷai niṟuvi, viññāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum ēṉaiya puttakaṅkaḷaiyum acciṭṭu veḷippaṭutti yirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta viññāṉa nūlkaḷai yām aṟintavaraiyil kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
pūkōḷa nūl
pūmi cāttiram: itai irēṉiyus aiyar 1832-il eḻutic ceṉṉaiyil acciṭṭār. itutāṉ mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa pūkōḷa nūl eṉat terikiṟatu. katri2 eṉpavar eḻutiya “ārampap pūkōḷam”3 eṉṉum nūlai ātāramākak koṇṭu ivar inta nūlai eḻutiṉār.
pūmi cāttirac curukkam (1846), pūmicāttirak kuṟippu. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷum nākarkōyil accukkūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭaṉa. pūmi cāttirap poḻippu ilaṅkaiyilum, pūmi cāttirap pāṭaṅkaḷ putuccēriyilum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟi, iṉṉum anēka pūmi cāttira nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ērāḷamāka veḷiyiṭap paṭṭaṉa.
tēca carittirak kataikaḷ
ulaka carittiram (1881), iṅkilāntu tēca carittiram (1858), intu tēca carittiram, pala tēca carittirac curukkam, urōma carittiram,4 ivai ceṉṉaip pāṭacālaip puttakac caṅkattārāl veḷiyiṭap paṭṭavai.
piriṭṭiṣ tēca carittiram (1855),5 purātaṉac carittirac carukkam,6 (1851) ivviru puttakaṅkaḷum revaraṇṭu hāps eṉpavarāl eḻutip pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai accuk kūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.
ulaka carittira mālai,7 (1830) itu ṭākṭar ṣimiṭ8 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭuc ceṉṉai ci.em.es. accuk kūṭattiṟ patippikkap paṭṭatu.
cātāraṇa itikācam9: iḵtu ārṉālṭ10 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, 1858-il yāḻppāṇattu amerikkaṉ micaṉ accuk kūṭattiṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu. pūrvika carittiram11 revaraṇṭu sārjaṇṭ eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭa ipputtakam 1850-il pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
intu cāti viḷakkam cārls caimaṉ eṉpavarāl eḻuti 1852-il ceṉṉaiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam mutaliyaṉa
vīca kaṇitam12: itu yāḻppāṇattavarāṉa kāral13 eṉpavarāl 1855-il eḻutappaṭṭu, yāḻppāṇattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam: itu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaic carc micaṉ uyartarap pāṭacālaiyiṉ talaimaiyāciriyarāyirunta ṭēviṭ cālamaṉ eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭatu. 1859-il acciṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam14 āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum eḻutap paṭṭa in nūl 1899-il ceṉṉaiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu. ec. hālim, em.ṭi. aṭaikkalam eṉpavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭatu.
iraṇṭu puttakaṅkaḷ15 kaṉam ji.yu. pōp aiyaravarkaḷāl acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai yārāl tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teriyavillai.
kṣēttirak kaṇitam16: itu sprāṭ17 eṉpavarāl pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
nila aḷavai nūl18: iḵtu 1858-il acciṭappaṭṭatu. moḻi peyarppāḷar iṉṉār eṉṟu teriyavillai.
iyaṟkaip poruḷ nūl, pirāṇi nūl mutaliyaṉa
palavakait tiruṭṭāntam19: itu potu aṟivu nūl. kaṉam irēṉiyus aiyarāl eḻutip pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
ūrtiri vilaṅkiyal (1836-varucam)20, vaṉavilaṅkiyal21 maccaviyal: ivai nākarkōyil accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.
niyāya ilakkaṇam: intat tarkka cāttiram yāḻp pāṇattavarāṉa villiyam neviṉs22 eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭu, yāḻppāṇattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
aṅkātipātam23: kirīṉ24 eṉpavar eḻutiyatu; 1872-il yāḻppāṇattil acciṭappaṭṭatu. arumaiyāṉa ciṟanta nūl.
maruntuc carakkukaḷiṉ peyar25: ituvum kirīṉ eṉpavar eḻutiyatu; 1875-il nākarkōyil accukkūṭattil acciṭappaṭṭatu.
iracāyaṉa mutal nūl26 pavar aiyarāl eḻutappaṭṭa racāyaṉa nūl. kemistam27: kirīṉ vaittiyar iyaṟṟiyatu. 1875-ām āṇṭil nākarkōyilil acciṭappaṭṭatu. vāṉa cāttiram, cukaraṇavātam, uṟpālaṉam eṉṉum nūlkaḷum yāḻppāṇattil acciṭap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa viññāṉa nūlkaḷiṉ muḻu vivaramum emakkuk kiṭaikkātapaṭiyāl, emakkut terintavaraiyil curukkamāka eḻutappaṭṭatu.

5. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum airōppiyat 
ticaic coṟkaḷ
vaḻakkāṟṟiluḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷilum ayal nāṭṭut ticaic coṟkaḷ kalantuviṭuvatu iyaṟkai. iḵtu ekkālattilum ellā nāṭṭilum ellā moḻikaḷilum ēṟpaṭṭuvarukiṟatu. ki.mu.1015-ām āṇṭil irunta cālamaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattilum, avaṉukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilum, tuṇi, tantam, kuraṅku, iñci, miḷaku, arici, mayil, cantaṉakkaṭṭai mutaliya poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tamiḻp peyarākavē epirēya1 moḻiyil vaḻaṅki vantatai “paipiḷ” eṉṉum viviliya nūliṟ kāṇalām. avvitamē, arici, iñcivēr pōṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kirīkku, ilattīṉ eṉṉum moḻikaḷiṟ citaintu vaḻaṅki vantataiyum kāṇkiṟōm. paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē intiyāvukku vanta āriyar pēciya āriyamoḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ pala kalantirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycci valla aṟivāḷarkaḷ collukiṟārkaḷ. āṅkila moḻiyilum kaṟi, kaṭṭumaram, miḷakuttaṇṇīr, kūli, veṟṟilai mutaliya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ citaintu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟē airōppiyat ticaic coṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu āṅkilam aracāṅka moḻiyāka iruppatu paṟṟiyum, āṅkilam kaṟṟavarkkē aluval kiṭaikkiṉṟamai paṟṟiyum, vaṇikat tuṟaiyilum āṅkilamoḻi vēṇṭappaṭukiṟatu paṟṟiyum nām āṅkilam kaṟṟuvarukiṟōm. ivvitam āṅkilat toṭar puṭaimaipaṟṟic cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷ tamiḻil, mutaṉmaiyākap pēccuvaḻakkiṟ payiṉṟuvarukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟaik kīḻē tarukiṟōm: kavarṉar, kōrṭṭu, āpīs, kāppi, raiṭṭar, paṭlar, jaṭj, pēppar, piṉalkōṭu, cammaṉ, cāppu, mōṭṭār, ṣarṭṭu, kōṭṭu, kampeṉi, carccu, micaṉ, rōṭṭu, kamiṭṭi, mīṭṭiṅku, ṭirāmkār, cōppu mutaliyaṉa. itu niṟka.
16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal namatu nāṭṭil vāṇikam ceytuvanta pōrccukīciyar, ollāntar, pireñcukkārar mutaliya airōppiya iṉattāriṉ moḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻiṟ kalantirukkavēṇṭu mallavā? itaippaṟṟi ārāyumiṭattup pōrccukīciya rākiya paṟaṅkiyar moḻic coṟkaḷ tavira, ēṉaiya ollāntu moḻic coṟkaḷum pireñcumoḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻiṟ kalantu vaḻaṅkiyatākat teriyavillai. pireñcu, ollāntu, ṭēṉiṣ moḻic coṟkaḷmaṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalakkāmal, pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalantatu putumaiyākat tōṉṟalām. 16,17,18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil namatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil nāṉku airōppiya iṉattār vantiruntu vāṇikam ceytirukka, annāṉku moḻic coṟkaḷum tamiḻil ticaic coṟkaḷākak kalakkāmal, pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum kalakkavēṇṭiya kāraṇam eṉṉa? kāraṇam ituvākum: mutaṉmutalil namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta airōppiya iṉattār pōrccukīciyarāvar. avarkaḷukkup piṟakutāṉ maṟṟa airōppiyac cātiyār iṅku vantaṉar. mutalil vanta pōrccukīcuc cātiyār namatu nāṭṭiṉiṟ cilaraik kiṟittavarākki, avarkaḷukkup pōrccukīcu moḻiyaik kaṟpittaṉar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōṭṭai kaḷaik kāppataṟkāka aḻaittuvanta cippāykaḷukku intiyap peṇ kaḷaik kaliyāṇam ceytuvaittaṉar. intak kalappu maṇattiṉāṟ piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷ ‘tuppācikaḷ’ eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa oru taṉi iṉattārāka iruntaṉar. intat tuppācikaḷ pōrccukīcu moḻiyait taṅkaḷ tāymoḻiyākap pēcivantaṉar. (ippoḻutu intiyar kaḷukkum āṅkilēyarukkum piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷ ‘āṅkilō intiyar’ eṉṉum peyarpeṟṟu, āṅkilamoḻiyait tāymoḻi yākak koṇṭiruppatupōla, pōrccukīciyarukkum intiyarukkum piṟanta tuppāci eṉṉum vakuppār pōrccukīcu moḻiyait tāymoḻiyākap pēcivantaṉar.) pōrccukīciyarukkup piṟaku namatu nāṭṭiṟku vanta maṟṟa airōppiyarum pōrccukīcu moḻiyaiyē akkālattil intiyāvil potumoḻiyākap pēcivantārkaḷ. airōppiya varttakar ellōrum pōrccukīcu moḻiyaip potumoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkivantatupaṟṟi, vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum piṟa kāraṇattiṉporuṭṭum avarkaḷuṭaṉ paḻakiya tamiḻarum ammoḻiyai akkālattiṟ kaṟkavēṇṭiyatu kaṭṭāyamāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka namatu nāṭṭiṟ pōrccukīcu moḻi oruvakaiyiṟ potumoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkivantapaṭiyāl, ammoḻic coṟkaḷmaṭṭum tamiḻiṟ kalakka, maṟṟa airōppiya moḻic coṟkaḷ kalakka iṭamillātapaṭi pōyiṉa. pōrccukīcu moḻic coṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvataṟku ituvē kāraṇamākum. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum pōrccukīcut ticaiccoṟkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavai eṉpatai ārāyntu yāḻppāṇattu nallūr uyartiru. ñāṉappirakāca cuvāmikaḷ, O.M.I., āṅkilap pattirikaiyoṉṟil2 oru kaṭṭurai eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. cuvāmikaḷiṉ uttaravu peṟṟu akkaṭṭuraiyiliruntu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟaik kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
aṉṉāci allatu aṉṉatāḻai, koyyā, pappāy (pappaḷi), vāttu, kuciṉi, pīṅkāṉ, kōppai, vicukkōttu,3 arakku,4 kamicu, cappāttu, pāppūcu, pottāṉ, aluppunētti, mēcai, katirai5 vāṅku6puṉal7, kaṭutāci, pēṉā, kirāti, virāntai,8 cimēntu9 pīppā, mēstiri, kappittāṉ, āyā (cevilittāy)10 pātiri, vicuppu11 pāppu12 paṭṭāḷam, turuppu, cippāy, tuppākki, vayiṉāttu,13 kumpācu, naṅkūram, cāvi, ēlam, racītu, ācupattiri, iskūl, kumpātiri,14 kummātiri,15 viviliyam,16 paṭṭācu, caṉṉal mutaliyaṉa.
uyartiru.ñāṉappirakāca cuvāmikaḷ ārāynteḻutiya paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷ attaṉaiyum iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭavillai. avarkaḷ eḻutiyavaṟṟil oru cilavaṟṟaimaṭṭum mēlē kuṟippiṭṭōm. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ellāp paṟaṅkic coṟkaḷaiyum aṟiya virumpuvōr mēlē coṉṉa āṅkilappattirikaiyaip pārttu aṟintu koḷḷalām. ap paṟaṅkit ticaiccoṟkaḷiṟ cila maṭṭum ellāt tamiḻarkaḷālum vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila coṟkaḷ kiṟittuvat tamiḻarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟuñ cila ilaṅkait tamiḻarkaḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil ippōtu vaḻak koḻintaṉavum cila uṇṭu.
